--- src/main/java/com/crypto/service/HigherTimeframeBuyAssessmentService.java ---
   1: package com.crypto.service;
   2: 
   3: import com.crypto.domain.SignalDecision;
   4: import com.crypto.domain.TradeSignal;
   5: import org.springframework.stereotype.Service;
   6: 
   7: /**
   8:  * Pure FIX-154 BUY-context assessment. It deliberately returns effective
   9:  * decisions separately and never mutates the JPA-managed context signals.
  10:  */
  11: @Service
  12: public class HigherTimeframeBuyAssessmentService {
  13:     public static final int HARD_CONFIDENCE_THRESHOLD = 70;
  14: 
  15:     public Assessment assess(TradeSignal fiveMinute, boolean fiveFresh,
  16:                              TradeSignal oneHour, boolean oneFresh) {
  17:         if (!fiveFresh) return Assessment.reject("MISSING_5M_CONFIRMATION", Band.NOT_APPLICABLE, fiveMinute, oneHour);
  18:         if (!oneFresh) return Assessment.reject("MISSING_1H_CONTEXT", Band.NOT_APPLICABLE, fiveMinute, oneHour);
  19: 
  20:         SignalDecision five = decision(fiveMinute);
  21:         SignalDecision one = decision(oneHour);
  22:         if (bearish(five)) {
  23:             boolean hard = hard(oneHour);
  24:             return Assessment.reject("5M_BEARISH_VETO", hard ? Band.HARD_BEARISH_CANDIDATE : band(oneHour), fiveMinute, oneHour)
  25:                     .withHardConfirmation(hard);
  26:         }
  27:         if (bearish(one) && !qualityValid(oneHour)) {
  28:             return Assessment.reject("1H_BEARISH_VETO", Band.NOT_TRUSTED, fiveMinute, oneHour);
  29:         }
  30:         if (hard(oneHour)) {
  31:             return Assessment.reject("HTF_TRUSTED_BEARISH_VETO", Band.HARD_BEARISH_CANDIDATE, fiveMinute, oneHour);
  32:         }
  33:         if (soft(oneHour)) {
  34:             return Assessment.allow(Band.SOFT_CONFLICT, five, SignalDecision.NEUTRAL, 25, fiveMinute, oneHour);
  35:         }
  36:         return Assessment.allow(qualityValid(oneHour) ? Band.NO_CONFLICT : Band.NOT_TRUSTED,
  37:                 five, one, null, fiveMinute, oneHour);
  38:     }
  39: 
  40:     private Band band(TradeSignal one) {
  41:         if (!qualityValid(one)) return Band.NOT_TRUSTED;
  42:         if (hard(one)) return Band.HARD_BEARISH_CANDIDATE;
  43:         if (soft(one)) return Band.SOFT_CONFLICT;
  44:         return Band.NO_CONFLICT;
  45:     }
  46:     private boolean hard(TradeSignal s) { return qualityValid(s) && decision(s) == SignalDecision.STRONG_SELL && confidence(s) >= HARD_CONFIDENCE_THRESHOLD; }
  47:     private boolean soft(TradeSignal s) { return qualityValid(s) && (decision(s) == SignalDecision.SELL || (decision(s) == SignalDecision.STRONG_SELL && confidence(s) < HARD_CONFIDENCE_THRESHOLD)); }
  48:     private boolean qualityValid(TradeSignal s) { return s != null && Boolean.TRUE.equals(s.getDataQualityValid()); }
  49:     private int confidence(TradeSignal s) { return s == null ? 0 : s.getConfidenceScore(); }
  50:     private SignalDecision decision(TradeSignal s) { return s == null || s.getDecision() == null ? SignalDecision.NEUTRAL : s.getDecision(); }
  51:     private boolean bearish(SignalDecision d) { return d == SignalDecision.SELL || d == SignalDecision.STRONG_SELL; }
  52: 
  53:     public enum Band { NOT_APPLICABLE, NOT_TRUSTED, SOFT_CONFLICT, HARD_BEARISH_CANDIDATE, NO_CONFLICT }
  54:     public record Assessment(boolean allowed, String code, Band band, SignalDecision effectiveFiveMinute,
  55:                              SignalDecision effectiveOneHour, Integer maxInitialPercent,
  56:                              boolean hardConfirmationPresent, TradeSignal rawFiveMinute, TradeSignal rawOneHour) {
  57:         static Assessment reject(String code, Band band, TradeSignal five, TradeSignal one) {
  58:             return new Assessment(false, code, band, decisionOf(five), decisionOf(one), null, false, five, one);
  59:         }
  60:         static Assessment allow(Band band, SignalDecision five, SignalDecision one, Integer cap, TradeSignal rawFive, TradeSignal rawOne) {
  61:             return new Assessment(true, "HTF_CONTEXT_ALLOWED", band, five, one, cap, false, rawFive, rawOne);
  62:         }
  63:         Assessment withHardConfirmation(boolean value) { return new Assessment(allowed, code, band, effectiveFiveMinute, effectiveOneHour, maxInitialPercent, value, rawFiveMinute, rawOneHour); }
  64:         private static SignalDecision decisionOf(TradeSignal s) { return s == null || s.getDecision() == null ? SignalDecision.NEUTRAL : s.getDecision(); }
  65:     }
  66: }

--- src/main/java/com/crypto/service/TradeExecutionValidationService.java ---
   1: package com.crypto.service;
   2: 
   3: import com.crypto.domain.BtcContextStatus;
   4: import com.crypto.domain.SignalDecision;
   5: import com.crypto.domain.TradeSignal;
   6: import com.crypto.repository.TradeSignalRepository;
   7: import com.crypto.execution.service.ExecutionReplayScope;
   8: import com.crypto.execution.service.EntryConsumptionPolicy;

  12: import com.crypto.wallet.repository.WalletSettingsRepository;
  13: import lombok.RequiredArgsConstructor;
  14: import lombok.extern.slf4j.Slf4j;
  15: import org.springframework.beans.factory.annotation.Autowired;
  16: import org.springframework.stereotype.Service;
  17: 
  18: import java.math.BigDecimal;
  19: import java.time.Duration;
  20: import java.time.Instant;
  21: 
  22: /**
  23:  * Execution-only multi-timeframe validation.
  24:  *
  25:  * Frozen responsibilities:
  26:  *  - 1m: the only normal execution timeframe
  27:  *  - 5m: tactical confirmation
  28:  *  - 1h: strategic filter/veto
  29:  *
  30:  * Execution profiles affect only entry eligibility and position percentage.
  31:  * They never modify indicators, scores, or FinalDecisionService output.
  32:  */
  33: @Slf4j
  34: @Service
  35: @RequiredArgsConstructor
  36: public class TradeExecutionValidationService {
  37: 
  38:     private static final String EXECUTION_INTERVAL = "1m";
  39:     private static final String CONFIRMATION_INTERVAL = "5m";
  40:     private static final String TREND_INTERVAL = "1h";
  41:     private static final Duration ONE_MINUTE_TRANSITION_MAX_AGE = Duration.ofMinutes(10);
  42:     private static final Duration FIVE_MINUTE_MAX_AGE = Duration.ofMinutes(20);
  43:     private static final Duration ONE_HOUR_MAX_AGE = Duration.ofHours(3);
  44: 
  45:     // FIX-11G: this mirrors the existing global CHASE_ENTRY_CUTOFF in
  46:     // ExecutionIntelligenceService. We deliberately reuse the proven execution-quality
  47:     // boundary instead of inventing a new confidence/quality threshold for this one route.
  48:     // The downstream global Entry Quality guard still remains authoritative and re-checks it.
  49:     private static final int BALANCED_TRANSITIONAL_MIN_ENTRY_QUALITY = 50;
  50: 
  51:     private final TradeSignalRepository signalRepository;
  52:     private final WalletSettingsRepository settingsRepository;
  53:     private final EntryConsumptionPolicy entryConsumptionPolicy;
  54:     private final HigherTimeframeBuyAssessmentService htfAssessment;
  55:     @Autowired(required = false)
  56:     private ExecutionReplayScope replayScope;
  57:     @Autowired(required = false)
  58:     private BuyHtfAssessmentAuditStore htfAuditStore;
  59: 
  60:     public ValidationResult validateBuy(TradeSignal executionSignal, int entryQualityScore) {
  61:         ValidationResult structural = validateBaseSignal(executionSignal, true);
  62:         if (!structural.allowed()) return structural;
  63: 
  64:         WalletSettings settings = settings();
  65:         if (settings.isRequireNewBuyTransition()) {
  66:             TradeSignal previous = previousSignal(executionSignal.getSymbol(), EXECUTION_INTERVAL, executionSignal.getGeneratedAt()).orElse(null);
  67:             // FIX-112A: a bullish label alone is not a consumed entry. Only keep
  68:             // BUY_CONTINUATION protection when that exact previous signal has an
  69:             // executed BUY (Production) or executed shadow BUY (Replay).
  70:             if (isFresh(previous, executionSignal.getGeneratedAt(), ONE_MINUTE_TRANSITION_MAX_AGE)
  71:                     && isBullish(previous.getDecision())
  72:                     && entryConsumptionPolicy.resolve(previous.getId()) != EntryConsumptionState.NOT_CONSUMED) {
  73:                 return ValidationResult.reject("BUY_CONTINUATION",
  74:                         "BUY was not executed because a recent bullish opportunity for this symbol was already consumed. "
  75:                                 + "A new WATCH/NEUTRAL/SELL -> BUY transition is required.");
  76:             }
  77:         }
  78: 
  79:         TradeSignal fiveMinute = latestAtOrBefore(executionSignal, CONFIRMATION_INTERVAL);
  80:         if (!isFresh(fiveMinute, executionSignal.getGeneratedAt(), FIVE_MINUTE_MAX_AGE)) {
  81:             return ValidationResult.reject("MISSING_5M_CONFIRMATION",
  82:                     "No fresh 5m confirmation was available for the 1m BUY signal.");
  83:         }
  84: 
  85:         TradeSignal oneHour = latestAtOrBefore(executionSignal, TREND_INTERVAL);
  86:         if (!isFresh(oneHour, executionSignal.getGeneratedAt(), ONE_HOUR_MAX_AGE)) {
  87:             return ValidationResult.reject("MISSING_1H_CONTEXT",
  88:                     "No fresh 1h strategic context was available for the 1m BUY signal.");
  89:         }
  90: 
  91:         HigherTimeframeBuyAssessmentService.Assessment assessment=htfAssessment.assess(fiveMinute,true,oneHour,true);
  92:         if(!assessment.allowed()) {
  93:             audit(executionSignal, "DIRECT_BUY", assessment, assessment.code(), "FIX-154 higher-timeframe assessment rejected this BUY.");
  94:             return ValidationResult.reject(assessment.code(), "FIX-154 higher-timeframe assessment rejected this BUY.");
  95:         }
  96: 
  97:         ExecutionProfile profile = ExecutionProfile.from(settings.getExecutionProfile());
  98:         ValidationResult profileResult = switch (profile) {
  99:             case CONSERVATIVE -> conservativeBuy(assessment.effectiveFiveMinute(), assessment.effectiveOneHour());
 100:             // FIX-112B: only the fresh direct-BUY BALANCED path receives the narrow
 101:             // 5m-NEUTRAL exception. validateBuyContext() and AGGRESSIVE fallback keep
 102:             // calling balancedBuy() directly so their proven Production behavior is unchanged.
 103:             case BALANCED -> balancedBuyWithNeutralFiveException(
 104:                     executionSignal, assessment.effectiveFiveMinute(), assessment.effectiveOneHour(), entryQualityScore);
 105:             case AGGRESSIVE -> aggressiveBuy(executionSignal, assessment.effectiveFiveMinute(), assessment.effectiveOneHour());
 106:         };
 107:         ValidationResult result = cap(profileResult, assessment);
 108:         audit(executionSignal, "DIRECT_BUY", assessment, result.code(), result.explanation());
 109:         return result;
 110:     }
 111: 
 112:     /**
 113:      * FIX-021 / accumulated-evidence authority parity:
 114:      * Validate only the current 5m/1h execution authority using the same configured
 115:      * execution profile as a normal direct BUY. This intentionally skips the 1m BUY
 116:      * transition requirement because accumulated evidence may be triggered by a WATCH,
 117:      * but it must never be allowed to use weaker higher-timeframe authority than the
 118:      * normal BUY path would accept.
 119:      */
 120:     public ValidationResult validateBuyContext(TradeSignal referenceSignal) {
 121:         if (referenceSignal == null || referenceSignal.getGeneratedAt() == null) {
 122:             return ValidationResult.reject("INVALID_SIGNAL", "Execution reference signal is missing required timing data.");
 123:         }
 124: 
 125:         TradeSignal fiveMinute = latestAtOrBefore(referenceSignal, CONFIRMATION_INTERVAL);
 126:         if (!isFresh(fiveMinute, referenceSignal.getGeneratedAt(), FIVE_MINUTE_MAX_AGE)) {
 127:             return ValidationResult.reject("MISSING_5M_CONFIRMATION",
 128:                     "No fresh 5m confirmation was available for accumulated-evidence authority.");
 129:         }
 130: 
 131:         TradeSignal oneHour = latestAtOrBefore(referenceSignal, TREND_INTERVAL);
 132:         if (!isFresh(oneHour, referenceSignal.getGeneratedAt(), ONE_HOUR_MAX_AGE)) {
 133:             return ValidationResult.reject("MISSING_1H_CONTEXT",
 134:                     "No fresh 1h strategic context was available for accumulated-evidence authority.");
 135:         }
 136: 
 137:         HigherTimeframeBuyAssessmentService.Assessment assessment=htfAssessment.assess(fiveMinute,true,oneHour,true);
 138:         if(!assessment.allowed()) {
 139:             audit(referenceSignal, "ACCUMULATED_EVIDENCE", assessment, assessment.code(), "FIX-154 higher-timeframe assessment rejected this BUY context.");
 140:             return ValidationResult.reject(assessment.code(), "FIX-154 higher-timeframe assessment rejected this BUY context.");
 141:         }
 142: 
 143:         ExecutionProfile profile = ExecutionProfile.from(settings().getExecutionProfile());
 144:         TradeSignal oneMinuteQuality = latestAtOrBefore(referenceSignal, EXECUTION_INTERVAL);
 145:         TradeSignal qualitySignal = oneMinuteQuality == null ? referenceSignal : oneMinuteQuality;
 146:         ValidationResult profileResult = switch (profile) {
 147:             case CONSERVATIVE -> conservativeBuy(assessment.effectiveFiveMinute(), assessment.effectiveOneHour());
 148:             case BALANCED -> balancedBuy(assessment.effectiveFiveMinute(), assessment.effectiveOneHour());
 149:             case AGGRESSIVE -> aggressiveBuy(qualitySignal, assessment.effectiveFiveMinute(), assessment.effectiveOneHour());
 150:         };
 151:         ValidationResult result = cap(profileResult, assessment);
 152:         audit(referenceSignal, "ACCUMULATED_EVIDENCE", assessment, result.code(), result.explanation());
 153:         return result;
 154:     }
 155: 
 156:     public ValidationResult validateSell(TradeSignal executionSignal) {
 157:         ValidationResult base = validateBaseSignal(executionSignal, false);
 158:         if (!base.allowed()) return base;
 159: 
 160:         TradeSignal fiveMinute = latestAtOrBefore(executionSignal, CONFIRMATION_INTERVAL);
 161:         if (!isFresh(fiveMinute, executionSignal.getGeneratedAt(), FIVE_MINUTE_MAX_AGE)) {
 162:             return ValidationResult.reject("MISSING_5M_CONFIRMATION",
 163:                     "No fresh 5m confirmation was available for the 1m SELL signal.");
 164:         }
 165:         if (!isBearish(fiveMinute.getDecision())) {
 166:             return ValidationResult.reject("5M_NOT_BEARISH",
 167:                     "1m SELL was blocked because the fresh 5m decision was " + fiveMinute.getDecision() + ".");
 168:         }
 169: 
 170:         TradeSignal oneHour = latestAtOrBefore(executionSignal, TREND_INTERVAL);
 171:         if (!isFresh(oneHour, executionSignal.getGeneratedAt(), ONE_HOUR_MAX_AGE)) {
 172:             return ValidationResult.reject("MISSING_1H_CONTEXT",
 173:                     "No fresh 1h strategic context was available for the 1m SELL signal.");
 174:         }
 175:         if (isBullish(oneHour.getDecision())) {
 176:             return ValidationResult.reject("1H_BULLISH_VETO",
 177:                     "1m SELL was blocked because the fresh 1h strategic decision was " + oneHour.getDecision() + ".");
 178:         }
 179: 
 180:         return ValidationResult.allow(100, "SELL_CONFIRMED",
 181:                 "1m SELL confirmed by fresh bearish 5m context; 1h is not bullish.");
 182:     }
 183: 
 184:     private ValidationResult conservativeBuy(SignalDecision fiveMinute, SignalDecision oneHour) {
 185:         if (!isBullish(fiveMinute)) {
 186:             return ValidationResult.reject("5M_NOT_BULLISH",
 187:                     "Conservative profile requires 5m BUY/STRONG_BUY; fresh 5m is " + fiveMinute + ".");
 188:         }
 189:         return ValidationResult.allow(100, "CONSERVATIVE_FULL",
 190:                 "Conservative profile approved a full-size entry: 1m BUY, bullish 5m confirmation, and non-bearish 1h context.");
 191:     }
 192: 
 193:     /**
 194:      * FIX-112B / Production behavior change:
 195:      * 5m NEUTRAL means short-term confirmation is absent, but it is not bearish
 196:      * opposition. After the existing BALANCED rules reject, allow only a small
 197:      * exploratory entry when the fresh 1m BUY is high-confidence, 1h is genuinely
 198:      * bullish, independent Entry Quality is strong, and BTC has no conflict state.
 199:      *
 200:      * Golden rule: Replay = Production. This method lives in the shared Production
 201:      * validation service and Replay must call this exact path with historical inputs;
 202:      * there is no Replay-only copy, threshold, or behavior fork.
 203:      *
 204:      * balancedBuy() intentionally remains unchanged. Existing BALANCED_FULL,
 205:      * BALANCED_STRONG and BALANCED_EARLY authority always wins before this exception.
 206:      * FIX-11G extends this same direct-BUY-only wrapper after FIX-112B with the distinct
 207:      * 5m=NEUTRAL + 1h=WATCH transitional authority documented below.
 208:      */
 209:     private ValidationResult balancedBuyWithNeutralFiveException(
 210:             TradeSignal executionSignal, SignalDecision fiveMinute, SignalDecision oneHour, int entryQualityScore) {
 211:         ValidationResult balanced = balancedBuy(fiveMinute, oneHour);
 212:         if (balanced.allowed()) return balanced;
 213: 
 214:         boolean neutralFiveBullishOne = fiveMinute == SignalDecision.NEUTRAL && isBullish(oneHour);
 215:         BtcContextStatus btcStatus = executionSignal.getBtcContextStatus();
 216:         // FIX-112B: plain/strong BTC conflict cannot be rescued by this exception.
 217:         // FIX-141: nor can a failed freshness guard - STALE_CONTEXT is a real, evidenced
 218:         // block (missing/stale/future-dated/unknown-candle-time BTC context), not an
 219:         // "unavailable, non-authoritative" state. This exception must not restore the
 220:         // entry permission the freshness guard already withheld upstream.
 221:         // Other BTC states retain their existing upstream Production semantics.
 222:         boolean btcSafeForException = btcStatus != BtcContextStatus.CONFLICT
 223:                 && btcStatus != BtcContextStatus.STRONG_CONFLICT
 224:                 && btcStatus != BtcContextStatus.STALE_CONTEXT;
 225:         boolean strongEnough = executionSignal.getConfidenceScore() >= 72
 226:                 && entryQualityScore >= 70
 227:                 && btcSafeForException;
 228: 
 229:         if (neutralFiveBullishOne && strongEnough) {
 230:             return ValidationResult.allow(25, "BALANCED_NEUTRAL_5M",
 231:                     "BALANCED_NEUTRAL_5M: 25% exploratory entry approved because 5m is NEUTRAL "
 232:                              + "rather than bearish while 1h remains " + oneHour
 233:                             + ". 1m=" + executionSignal.getDecision()
 234:                             + ", confidence=" + executionSignal.getConfidenceScore()
 235:                              + ", 5m=NEUTRAL, 1h=" + oneHour
 236:                             + ", entryQuality=" + entryQualityScore
 237:                             + ", btcStatus=" + btcStatus
 238:                             + ", position=25%.");
 239:         }
 240: 
 241:         // FIX-11G / controlled Production behavior change:
 242:         // An already-approved fresh 1m BUY can appear before tactical 5m confirmation
 243:         // catches up. When 5m is still NEUTRAL and 1h is WATCH, neither timeframe is
 244:         // bearish, but the original BALANCED matrix has no authority for this transition.
 245:         // Grant only a 25% exploratory authority and only when FinalDecision already
 246:         // allowed entry, BTC has no hard conflict, and Entry Quality is at/above the
 247:         // existing chase cutoff. Do NOT add a second confidence threshold here: the
 248:         // upstream BUY + finalEntryAllowed decision already owns signal confidence.
 249:         //
 250:         // Golden rule: Replay = Production. Replay reaches this exact shared method
 251:         // through ExecutionReplayScope historical 5m/1h lookups; there is no Replay-only
 252:         // copy, threshold, or special execution behavior. validateBuyContext() remains
 253:         // intentionally unchanged so accumulated-evidence/recovery authority is not widened.
 254:         boolean neutralFiveWatchOne = fiveMinute == SignalDecision.NEUTRAL && oneHour == SignalDecision.WATCH;
 255:         if (neutralFiveWatchOne) {
 256:             boolean upstreamApproved = executionSignal.isFinalEntryAllowed();
 257:             boolean entryQualitySafe = entryQualityScore >= BALANCED_TRANSITIONAL_MIN_ENTRY_QUALITY;
 258:             // FIX-141: see btcSafeForException above - STALE_CONTEXT must not be rescued here either.
 259:             boolean btcSafeForTransitionalAuthority = btcStatus != BtcContextStatus.CONFLICT
 260:                     && btcStatus != BtcContextStatus.STRONG_CONFLICT
 261:                     && btcStatus != BtcContextStatus.STALE_CONTEXT;
 262: 
 263:             if (upstreamApproved && entryQualitySafe && btcSafeForTransitionalAuthority) {
 264:                 log.info("FIX-11G transitional BUY authority granted: signalId={}, symbol={}, generatedAt={}, "
 265:                                 + "decision={}, confidence={}, 5m={}, 1h={}, entryQuality={}, btcStatus={}, positionPercent=25",
 266:                         executionSignal.getId(), executionSignal.getSymbol(), executionSignal.getGeneratedAt(),
 267:                         executionSignal.getDecision(), executionSignal.getConfidenceScore(),
 268:                         fiveMinute, oneHour, entryQualityScore, btcStatus);
 269:                 return ValidationResult.allow(25, "BALANCED_NEUTRAL_5M_WATCH_1H",
 270:                         "BALANCED_NEUTRAL_5M_WATCH_1H: 25% exploratory entry approved for an already-approved "
 271:                                 + "1m BUY while 5m is NEUTRAL and 1h is WATCH. "
 272:                                 + "entryQuality=" + entryQualityScore
 273:                                 + ", btcStatus=" + btcStatus
 274:                                 + ", position=25%.");
 275:             }
 276: 
 277:             // INFO is intentional here: this exact transition is the new FIX-11G boundary.
 278:             // Keeping both grant and denial searchable by FIX-11G makes Production misses
 279:             // diagnosable from application logs without enabling global DEBUG logging.
 280:             log.info("FIX-11G transitional BUY authority not granted: signalId={}, symbol={}, generatedAt={}, "
 281:                             + "decision={}, confidence={}, finalEntryAllowed={}, 5m={}, 1h={}, entryQuality={}, "
 282:                             + "minEntryQuality={}, btcStatus={}",
 283:                     executionSignal.getId(), executionSignal.getSymbol(), executionSignal.getGeneratedAt(),
 284:                     executionSignal.getDecision(), executionSignal.getConfidenceScore(), upstreamApproved,
 285:                     fiveMinute, oneHour, entryQualityScore,
 286:                     BALANCED_TRANSITIONAL_MIN_ENTRY_QUALITY, btcStatus);
 287:         }
 288:         return balanced;
 289:     }
 290: 
 291:     private ValidationResult balancedBuy(SignalDecision five, SignalDecision one) {
 292: 
 293:         if (isBullish(five) && isBullish(one)) {
 294:             return ValidationResult.allow(100, "BALANCED_FULL",
 295:                     "Balanced profile approved 100%: both 5m and 1h are bullish.");
 296:         }
 297:         if (isBullish(five) && isWatchOrNeutral(one)) {
 298:             return ValidationResult.allow(75, "BALANCED_STRONG",
 299:                     "Balanced profile approved 75%: 5m is bullish while 1h is " + one + ".");
 300:         }
 301:         if (five == SignalDecision.WATCH && isBullish(one)) {
 302:             return ValidationResult.allow(75, "BALANCED_STRONG",
 303:                     "Balanced profile approved 75%: 1h is bullish while 5m is WATCH.");
 304:         }
 305:         if (five == SignalDecision.WATCH && one == SignalDecision.WATCH) {
 306:             return ValidationResult.allow(50, "BALANCED_EARLY",
 307:                     "Balanced profile approved 50%: both 5m and 1h are WATCH, so this is an early reduced entry.");
 308:         }
 309: 
 310:         return ValidationResult.reject("BALANCED_CONFIRMATION_INSUFFICIENT",
 311:                 "Balanced profile requires bullish/WATCH confirmation. Current 5m=" + five + ", 1h=" + one + ".");
 312:     }
 313: 
 314:     private ValidationResult aggressiveBuy(TradeSignal executionSignal, SignalDecision five, SignalDecision one) {
 315:         ValidationResult balanced = balancedBuy(five, one);
 316:         if (balanced.allowed()) return balanced;
 317: 
 318:         boolean probeAlignment = five == SignalDecision.WATCH && one == SignalDecision.NEUTRAL;
 319:         if (!probeAlignment) {
 320:             return ValidationResult.reject("AGGRESSIVE_CONFIRMATION_INSUFFICIENT",
 321:                     "Aggressive profile still requires 5m WATCH or better and a non-bearish 1h context. Current 5m="
 322:                             + five + ", 1h=" + one + ".");
 323:         }
 324: 
 325:         int confidence = executionSignal.getConfidenceScore();
 326:         int trend = executionSignal.getTrendScore();
 327:         int volume = executionSignal.getVolumeScore();
 328:         int momentum = executionSignal.getMomentumScore();
 329: 
 330:         if (confidence < 85 || trend < 22 || volume < 16 || momentum < 12) {
 331:             return ValidationResult.reject("AGGRESSIVE_PROBE_QUALITY",
 332:                     "Aggressive 25% probe requires confidence>=85, trend>=22, volume>=16, momentum>=12. "
 333:                             + "Actual confidence=" + confidence + ", trend=" + trend + ", volume=" + volume
 334:                             + ", momentum=" + momentum + ".");
 335:         }
 336: 
 337:         return ValidationResult.allow(25, "AGGRESSIVE_PROBE",
 338:                 "Aggressive profile approved a 25% probe: 1m BUY is high quality, 5m is WATCH, and 1h is NEUTRAL.");
 339:     }
 340: 
 341:     private ValidationResult validateBaseSignal(TradeSignal signal, boolean buy) {
 342:         if (signal == null || signal.getGeneratedAt() == null) {
 343:             return ValidationResult.reject("INVALID_SIGNAL", "Execution signal is missing required timing data.");
 344:         }
 345:         if (!EXECUTION_INTERVAL.equals(signal.getInterval())) {
 346:             return ValidationResult.reject("NON_EXECUTION_TIMEFRAME",
 347:                     "Only 1m signals may trigger normal wallet execution. " + signal.getInterval() + " is context-only.");
 348:         }
 349:         if (buy && !isBullish(signal.getDecision())) {
 350:             return ValidationResult.reject("NOT_BUY", "The 1m decision is not BUY/STRONG_BUY.");
 351:         }
 352:         if (!buy && !isBearish(signal.getDecision())) {
 353:             return ValidationResult.reject("NOT_SELL", "The 1m decision is not SELL/STRONG_SELL.");
 354:         }
 355:         return ValidationResult.allow(100, "BASE_VALID", "Base execution signal is valid.");
 356:     }
 357: 
 358:     private TradeSignal latestAtOrBefore(TradeSignal signal, String interval) {
 359:         return latestSignal(signal.getSymbol(), interval, signal.getGeneratedAt()).orElse(null);
 360:     }
 361: 
 362:     private java.util.Optional<TradeSignal> previousSignal(String symbol, String interval, Instant reference) {
 363:         if (replayScope != null && replayScope.active()) return replayScope.previousBefore(symbol, interval, reference);
 364:         return signalRepository.findTopBySymbolAndIntervalAndGeneratedAtLessThanOrderByGeneratedAtDesc(symbol, interval, reference);
 365:     }
 366: 
 367:     private java.util.Optional<TradeSignal> latestSignal(String symbol, String interval, Instant reference) {
 368:         if (replayScope != null && replayScope.active()) return replayScope.latestAtOrBefore(symbol, interval, reference);
 369:         return signalRepository.findTopBySymbolAndIntervalAndGeneratedAtLessThanEqualOrderByGeneratedAtDesc(symbol, interval, reference);
 370:     }
 371: 
 372:     private boolean isFresh(TradeSignal signal, Instant reference, Duration maximumAge) {
 373:         return signal != null
 374:                 && signal.getGeneratedAt() != null
 375:                 && !signal.getGeneratedAt().isAfter(reference)
 376:                 && !signal.getGeneratedAt().isBefore(reference.minus(maximumAge));
 377:     }
 378: 
 379:     private boolean isBullish(SignalDecision decision) {
 380:         return decision == SignalDecision.BUY || decision == SignalDecision.STRONG_BUY;
 381:     }
 382: 
 383:     private boolean isBearish(SignalDecision decision) {
 384:         return decision == SignalDecision.SELL || decision == SignalDecision.STRONG_SELL;
 385:     }
 386: 
 387:     private boolean isWatchOrNeutral(SignalDecision decision) {
 388:         return decision == SignalDecision.WATCH || decision == SignalDecision.NEUTRAL;
 389:     }
 390: 
 391:     private WalletSettings settings() {
 392:         return settingsRepository.findById(1L)
 393:                 .orElseGet(() -> WalletSettings.builder()
 394:                         .id(1L)
 395:                         .executionProfile(ExecutionProfile.BALANCED.name())
 396:                         .requireNewBuyTransition(true)
 397:                         .dynamicProfitLockEnabled(true)
 398:                         .profitLockActivationPercent(BigDecimal.valueOf(70))
 399:                         .profitLockInitialPercent(BigDecimal.valueOf(40))
 400:                         .profitLockTrailStepPercent(BigDecimal.valueOf(10))
 401:                         .build());
 402:     }
 403: 
 404:     private ValidationResult cap(ValidationResult result, HigherTimeframeBuyAssessmentService.Assessment assessment) {
 405:         if (!result.allowed() || assessment.maxInitialPercent()==null) return result;
 406:         int capped=Math.min(result.positionPercent(),assessment.maxInitialPercent());
 407:         return capped==result.positionPercent()?result:ValidationResult.allow(capped,result.code(),result.explanation()+" FIX-154 soft-HTF cap="+capped+"%.");
 408:     }
 409: 
 410:     /** Used only after progressive raw-HTF checks have already failed; never changes an add decision. */
 411:     public void recordProgressiveAssessmentIfSoftBlocked(TradeSignal referenceSignal) {
 412:         if (referenceSignal == null || referenceSignal.getGeneratedAt() == null) return;
 413:         TradeSignal five = latestAtOrBefore(referenceSignal, CONFIRMATION_INTERVAL);
 414:         TradeSignal one = latestAtOrBefore(referenceSignal, TREND_INTERVAL);
 415:         if (!isFresh(five, referenceSignal.getGeneratedAt(), FIVE_MINUTE_MAX_AGE)
 416:                 || !isFresh(one, referenceSignal.getGeneratedAt(), ONE_HOUR_MAX_AGE)) return;
 417:         HigherTimeframeBuyAssessmentService.Assessment assessment = htfAssessment.assess(five, true, one, true);
 418:         if (assessment.band() == HigherTimeframeBuyAssessmentService.Band.SOFT_CONFLICT) {
 419:             audit(referenceSignal, "PROGRESSIVE_ADD", assessment, "SOFT_HTF_ADD_BLOCKED",
 420:                     "Existing raw 1h progressive-add guard blocked this add; FIX-154 audit only.");
 421:         }
 422:     }
 423: 
 424:     private void audit(TradeSignal referenceSignal, String path,
 425:                        HigherTimeframeBuyAssessmentService.Assessment assessment, String finalResult, String reason) {
 426:         if (htfAuditStore != null) htfAuditStore.record(referenceSignal, path, assessment, finalResult, reason);
 427:     }
 428: 
 429:     public record ValidationResult(boolean allowed, String code, String explanation, int positionPercent) {
 430:         public static ValidationResult allow(int positionPercent, String code, String explanation) {
 431:             return new ValidationResult(true, code, explanation, Math.max(1, Math.min(100, positionPercent)));
 432:         }
 433: 
 434:         public static ValidationResult reject(String code, String explanation) {
 435:             return new ValidationResult(false, code, explanation, 0);
 436:         }
 437:     }
 438: }

--- src/main/java/com/crypto/service/OpportunityConsolidationService.java ---
   1: package com.crypto.service;
   2: 
   3: import com.crypto.domain.SignalDecision;
   4: import com.crypto.domain.TradeSignal;
   5: import com.crypto.repository.TradeSignalRepository;
   6: import com.crypto.execution.service.ExecutionReplayScope;
   7: import lombok.RequiredArgsConstructor;
   8: import org.springframework.beans.factory.annotation.Autowired;
   9: import org.springframework.stereotype.Service;
  10: 
  11: import java.time.Duration;
  12: import java.time.Instant;
  13: import java.util.ArrayList;
  14: import java.util.List;
  15: 
  16: /**
  17:  * Builds execution evidence from a sequence of already-generated 1m BUY signals.
  18:  *
  19:  * Important architecture rule: this service NEVER creates a TradeSignal.  AnalysisService
  20:  * remains the single source of truth for signals.  Consolidation is an execution-only path
  21:  * that may approve the CURRENT fresh 1m BUY after several recent BUY signals have persisted.
  22:  */
  23: @Service
  24: @RequiredArgsConstructor
  25: public class OpportunityConsolidationService {
  26: 
  27:     private static final String EXECUTION_INTERVAL = "1m";
  28:     private static final String CONFIRMATION_INTERVAL = "5m";
  29:     private static final String TREND_INTERVAL = "1h";
  30: 
  31:     private static final Duration OPPORTUNITY_WINDOW = Duration.ofMinutes(30);
  32:     private static final Duration FIVE_MINUTE_MAX_AGE = Duration.ofMinutes(20);
  33:     private static final Duration ONE_HOUR_MAX_AGE = Duration.ofHours(3);
  34: 
  35:     private static final int MIN_CONSECUTIVE_BUYS = 3;
  36:     private static final int STRONG_CONSECUTIVE_BUYS = 5;
  37:     private static final int MIN_AVERAGE_SCORE = 75;
  38:     private static final int MIN_AVERAGE_CONFIDENCE = 70;
  39: 
  40:     private final TradeSignalRepository signalRepository;
  41:     private final HigherTimeframeBuyAssessmentService htfAssessment;
  42:     @Autowired(required = false)
  43:     private ExecutionReplayScope replayScope;
  44:     @Autowired(required = false)
  45:     private BuyHtfAssessmentAuditStore htfAuditStore;
  46: 
  47:     public Assessment evaluate(TradeSignal currentSignal) {
  48:         if (currentSignal == null || currentSignal.getGeneratedAt() == null) {
  49:             return Assessment.reject("INVALID_SIGNAL", "Current execution signal is missing.");
  50:         }
  51:         if (!EXECUTION_INTERVAL.equals(currentSignal.getInterval()) || !isBullish(currentSignal.getDecision())) {
  52:             return Assessment.reject("NOT_1M_BUY", "Opportunity consolidation only evaluates fresh 1m BUY/STRONG_BUY signals.");
  53:         }
  54: 
  55:         TradeSignal fiveMinute = latestAtOrBefore(currentSignal, CONFIRMATION_INTERVAL);
  56:         TradeSignal oneHour = latestAtOrBefore(currentSignal, TREND_INTERVAL);
  57: 
  58:         if (!isFresh(fiveMinute, currentSignal.getGeneratedAt(), FIVE_MINUTE_MAX_AGE)) {
  59:             return Assessment.reject("MISSING_5M_CONTEXT", "No fresh 5m context is available for consolidation.");
  60:         }
  61:         if (!isFresh(oneHour, currentSignal.getGeneratedAt(), ONE_HOUR_MAX_AGE)) {
  62:             return Assessment.reject("MISSING_1H_CONTEXT", "No fresh 1h context is available for consolidation.");
  63:         }
  64:         HigherTimeframeBuyAssessmentService.Assessment htf=htfAssessment.assess(fiveMinute,true,oneHour,true);
  65:         if(!htf.allowed()) {
  66:             audit(currentSignal, htf, htf.code(), "FIX-154 higher-timeframe assessment rejected consolidation.");
  67:             return Assessment.reject(htf.code(), "FIX-154 higher-timeframe assessment rejected consolidation.");
  68:         }
  69:         SignalDecision fiveDecision=htf.effectiveFiveMinute();
  70:         SignalDecision oneDecision=htf.effectiveOneHour();
  71: 
  72:         List<TradeSignal> recent = replayScope != null && replayScope.active()
  73:                 ? replayScope.recent(currentSignal.getSymbol(), EXECUTION_INTERVAL, currentSignal.getGeneratedAt(), 20)
  74:                 : signalRepository.findTop20BySymbolAndIntervalOrderByGeneratedAtDesc(currentSignal.getSymbol(), EXECUTION_INTERVAL);
  75:         List<TradeSignal> consecutive = consecutiveFreshBuys(recent, currentSignal.getGeneratedAt());
  76: 
  77:         int count = consecutive.size();
  78:         if (count < MIN_CONSECUTIVE_BUYS) {
  79:             Assessment result = Assessment.building(
  80:                     count,
  81:                     averageScore(consecutive),
  82:                     averageConfidence(consecutive),
  83:                     firstScore(consecutive),
  84:                     currentSignal.getTotalScore(),
  85:                     fiveDecision,
  86:                     oneDecision,
  87:                     "BUILDING",
  88:                     "Bullish opportunity is building: " + count + "/" + MIN_CONSECUTIVE_BUYS
  89:                             + " consecutive fresh 1m BUY signals."
  90:             );
  91:             audit(currentSignal, htf, result.code(), result.explanation());
  92:             return result;
  93:         }
  94: 
  95:         int averageScore = averageScore(consecutive);
  96:         int averageConfidence = averageConfidence(consecutive);
  97:         int firstScore = firstScore(consecutive);
  98:         int latestScore = currentSignal.getTotalScore();
  99:         boolean scoreStableOrImproving = latestScore >= firstScore - 3;
 100: 
 101:         if (averageScore < MIN_AVERAGE_SCORE || averageConfidence < MIN_AVERAGE_CONFIDENCE || !scoreStableOrImproving) {
 102:             Assessment result = Assessment.rejectDetailed(
 103:                     "QUALITY_DECAY",
 104:                     count,
 105:                     averageScore,
 106:                     averageConfidence,
 107:                     firstScore,
 108:                     latestScore,
 109:                     fiveDecision,
 110:                     oneDecision,
 111:                     "Persistent BUY signals exist, but quality is not stable enough. Average score=" + averageScore
 112:                             + ", average confidence=" + averageConfidence + ", first/latest score=" + firstScore + "/" + latestScore + "."
 113:             );
 114:             audit(currentSignal, htf, result.code(), result.explanation());
 115:             return result;
 116:         }
 117: 
 118:         // Fully neutral 5m + 1h needs stronger persistence; this avoids treating repeated 1m noise as confirmation.
 119:         if (fiveDecision == SignalDecision.NEUTRAL
 120:                 && oneDecision == SignalDecision.NEUTRAL
 121:                 && count < STRONG_CONSECUTIVE_BUYS) {
 122:             Assessment result = Assessment.building(
 123:                     count,
 124:                     averageScore,
 125:                     averageConfidence,
 126:                     firstScore,
 127:                     latestScore,
 128:                     fiveDecision,
 129:                     oneDecision,
 130:                     "BUILDING_STRONGER_CONFIRMATION",
 131:                     "1m BUY persistence is valid, but both 5m and 1h are NEUTRAL. Waiting for "
 132:                             + STRONG_CONSECUTIVE_BUYS + " consecutive BUY signals or improving higher-timeframe context."
 133:             );
 134:             audit(currentSignal, htf, result.code(), result.explanation());
 135:             return result;
 136:         }
 137: 
 138:         int positionPercent = consolidatedPositionPercent(count, fiveDecision, oneDecision);
 139:         if(htf.maxInitialPercent()!=null) positionPercent=Math.min(positionPercent,htf.maxInitialPercent());
 140:         String state = count >= STRONG_CONSECUTIVE_BUYS ? "CONFIRMED" : "BUILDING_CONFIDENCE";
 141:         String explanation = "Consolidated opportunity approved from the current signal #" + currentSignal.getId()
 142:                 + ": " + count + " consecutive fresh 1m BUY signals, average score=" + averageScore
 143:                 + ", average confidence=" + averageConfidence + ", score path=" + firstScore + "ƒ+'" + latestScore
 144:                 + ", 5m=" + fiveDecision + ", 1h=" + oneDecision
 145:                 + ". Reduced execution size=" + positionPercent + "%. No second trade signal was generated.";
 146: 
 147:         Assessment result = Assessment.allow(
 148:                 positionPercent,
 149:                 count,
 150:                 averageScore,
 151:                 averageConfidence,
 152:                 firstScore,
 153:                 latestScore,
 154:                 fiveDecision,
 155:                 oneDecision,
 156:                 state,
 157:                 explanation
 158:         );
 159:         audit(currentSignal, htf, result.code(), result.explanation());
 160:         return result;
 161:     }
 162: 
 163:     private void audit(TradeSignal reference, HigherTimeframeBuyAssessmentService.Assessment assessment,
 164:                        String finalResult, String reason) {
 165:         if (htfAuditStore != null) htfAuditStore.record(reference, "CONSOLIDATION", assessment, finalResult, reason);
 166:     }
 167: 
 168:     private List<TradeSignal> consecutiveFreshBuys(List<TradeSignal> recent, Instant reference) {
 169:         List<TradeSignal> result = new ArrayList<>();
 170:         Instant cutoff = reference.minus(OPPORTUNITY_WINDOW);
 171:         for (TradeSignal signal : recent) {
 172:             if (signal.getGeneratedAt() == null || signal.getGeneratedAt().isAfter(reference)) {
 173:                 continue;
 174:             }
 175:             if (signal.getGeneratedAt().isBefore(cutoff)) {
 176:                 break;
 177:             }
 178:             if (!isBullish(signal.getDecision())) {
 179:                 break;
 180:             }
 181:             result.add(signal);
 182:         }
 183:         return result;
 184:     }
 185: 
 186:     private int consolidatedPositionPercent(int count, SignalDecision fiveMinute, SignalDecision oneHour) {
 187:         int percent = count >= STRONG_CONSECUTIVE_BUYS ? 50 : 25;
 188:         if (fiveMinute == SignalDecision.WATCH) percent += 10;
 189:         if (isBullish(fiveMinute)) percent += 20;
 190:         if (oneHour == SignalDecision.WATCH) percent += 5;
 191:         if (isBullish(oneHour)) percent += 10;
 192:         return Math.min(75, percent);
 193:     }
 194: 
 195:     private int averageScore(List<TradeSignal> signals) {
 196:         if (signals.isEmpty()) return 0;
 197:         return (int) Math.round(signals.stream().mapToInt(TradeSignal::getTotalScore).average().orElse(0));
 198:     }
 199: 
 200:     private int averageConfidence(List<TradeSignal> signals) {
 201:         if (signals.isEmpty()) return 0;
 202:         return (int) Math.round(signals.stream().mapToInt(TradeSignal::getConfidenceScore).average().orElse(0));
 203:     }
 204: 
 205:     private int firstScore(List<TradeSignal> signals) {
 206:         return signals.isEmpty() ? 0 : signals.get(signals.size() - 1).getTotalScore();
 207:     }
 208: 
 209:     private TradeSignal latestAtOrBefore(TradeSignal executionSignal, String interval) {
 210:         return (replayScope != null && replayScope.active()
 211:                 ? replayScope.latestAtOrBefore(executionSignal.getSymbol(), interval, executionSignal.getGeneratedAt())
 212:                 : signalRepository.findTopBySymbolAndIntervalAndGeneratedAtLessThanEqualOrderByGeneratedAtDesc(
 213:                         executionSignal.getSymbol(), interval, executionSignal.getGeneratedAt()))
 214:                 .orElse(null);
 215:     }
 216: 
 217:     private boolean isFresh(TradeSignal signal, Instant reference, Duration maximumAge) {
 218:         return signal != null
 219:                 && signal.getGeneratedAt() != null
 220:                 && !signal.getGeneratedAt().isAfter(reference)
 221:                 && !signal.getGeneratedAt().isBefore(reference.minus(maximumAge));
 222:     }
 223: 
 224:     private boolean isBullish(SignalDecision decision) {
 225:         return decision == SignalDecision.BUY || decision == SignalDecision.STRONG_BUY;
 226:     }
 227: 
 228:     private boolean isBearish(SignalDecision decision) {
 229:         return decision == SignalDecision.SELL || decision == SignalDecision.STRONG_SELL;
 230:     }
 231: 
 232:     public record Assessment(
 233:             boolean allowed,
 234:             String code,
 235:             String state,
 236:             String explanation,
 237:             int positionPercent,
 238:             int consecutiveBuyCount,
 239:             int averageScore,
 240:             int averageConfidence,
 241:             int firstScore,
 242:             int latestScore,
 243:             SignalDecision fiveMinuteDecision,
 244:             SignalDecision oneHourDecision
 245:     ) {
 246:         static Assessment allow(int positionPercent, int count, int averageScore, int averageConfidence,
 247:                                 int firstScore, int latestScore, SignalDecision fiveMinute, SignalDecision oneHour,
 248:                                 String state, String explanation) {
 249:             return new Assessment(true, "CONSOLIDATED_BUY", state, explanation,
 250:                     Math.max(1, Math.min(75, positionPercent)), count, averageScore, averageConfidence,
 251:                     firstScore, latestScore, fiveMinute, oneHour);
 252:         }
 253: 
 254:         static Assessment building(int count, int averageScore, int averageConfidence,
 255:                                    int firstScore, int latestScore, SignalDecision fiveMinute, SignalDecision oneHour,
 256:                                    String state, String explanation) {
 257:             return new Assessment(false, "OPPORTUNITY_BUILDING", state, explanation, 0,
 258:                     count, averageScore, averageConfidence, firstScore, latestScore, fiveMinute, oneHour);
 259:         }
 260: 
 261:         static Assessment reject(String code, String explanation) {
 262:             return new Assessment(false, code, "CANCELLED", explanation, 0,
 263:                     0, 0, 0, 0, 0, null, null);
 264:         }
 265: 
 266:         static Assessment rejectDetailed(String code, int count, int averageScore, int averageConfidence,
 267:                                          int firstScore, int latestScore, SignalDecision fiveMinute,
 268:                                          SignalDecision oneHour, String explanation) {
 269:             return new Assessment(false, code, "WEAKENING", explanation, 0,
 270:                     count, averageScore, averageConfidence, firstScore, latestScore, fiveMinute, oneHour);
 271:         }
 272:     }
 273: }

   1: package com.crypto.service;
   2: 
   3: import com.crypto.domain.TradeSignal;
   4: import lombok.extern.slf4j.Slf4j;
   5: import org.springframework.jdbc.core.JdbcTemplate;
   6: import org.springframework.stereotype.Component;
   7: import org.springframework.transaction.annotation.Propagation;
   8: import org.springframework.transaction.annotation.Transactional;
   9: 
  10: import java.sql.Timestamp;
  11: import java.time.Duration;
  12: import java.time.Instant;
  13: 
  14: /**
  15:  * Best-effort, append-only FIX-154 evidence.  It is deliberately outside the
  16:  * BUY decision: an unavailable audit table or failed insert must never reject
  17:  * a trade or roll back its execution transaction.
  18:  */
  19: @Slf4j
  20: @Component
  21: public class BuyHtfAssessmentAuditStore {
  22:     private final JdbcTemplate jdbc;
  23: 
  24:     public BuyHtfAssessmentAuditStore(JdbcTemplate jdbc) {
  25:         this.jdbc = jdbc;
  26:     }
  27: 
  28:     @Transactional(propagation = Propagation.REQUIRES_NEW)
  29:     public void record(TradeSignal reference, String path,
  30:                        HigherTimeframeBuyAssessmentService.Assessment assessment,
  31:                        String finalResult, String reason) {
  32:         if (reference == null || assessment == null) return;
  33:         try {
  34:             TradeSignal five = assessment.rawFiveMinute();
  35:             TradeSignal one = assessment.rawOneHour();
  36:             Instant evaluatedAt = reference.getGeneratedAt() == null ? Instant.now() : reference.getGeneratedAt();
  37:             jdbc.update("""
  38:                     INSERT INTO buy_htf_assessment_audit (
  39:                       reference_signal_id, evaluation_path, evaluated_at, replay_mode,
  40:                       one_hour_signal_id, one_hour_raw_decision, one_hour_effective_decision,
  41:                       one_hour_candle_close_time, one_hour_age_seconds, one_hour_data_quality_valid, one_hour_usability_reason,
  42:                       five_minute_signal_id, five_minute_raw_decision, five_minute_effective_decision,
  43:                       five_minute_candle_close_time, five_minute_age_seconds, five_minute_data_quality_valid, five_minute_usability_reason,
  44:                       confidence_threshold, assessment_band, max_initial_percent, legacy_veto_result, final_result, final_reason)
  45:                     VALUES (?, ?, ?, FALSE, ?, ?, ?, ?, ?, ?, ?, ?, ?, ?, ?, ?, ?, ?, ?, ?, ?, ?, ?, ?)
  46:                     """,
  47:                     reference.getId(), path, Timestamp.from(evaluatedAt),
  48:                     id(one), decision(one), assessment.effectiveOneHour(), generated(one), age(evaluatedAt, one), quality(one), assessment.band().name(),
  49:                     id(five), decision(five), assessment.effectiveFiveMinute(), generated(five), age(evaluatedAt, five), quality(five), fiveReason(five),
  50:                     HigherTimeframeBuyAssessmentService.HARD_CONFIDENCE_THRESHOLD, assessment.band().name(), assessment.maxInitialPercent(),
  51:                     "5M_BEARISH_VETO".equals(assessment.code()) ? assessment.code() : null, finalResult, trim(reason));
  52:         } catch (Exception e) {
  53:             log.warn("FIX-154 assessment audit was not recorded; BUY decision remains unchanged: path={}, referenceSignalId={}",
  54:                     path, reference.getId(), e);
  55:         }
  56:     }
  57: 
  58:     private static Long id(TradeSignal signal) { return signal == null ? null : signal.getId(); }
  59:     private static String decision(TradeSignal signal) { return signal == null || signal.getDecision() == null ? null : signal.getDecision().name(); }
  60:     private static Timestamp generated(TradeSignal signal) { return signal == null || signal.getGeneratedAt() == null ? null : Timestamp.from(signal.getGeneratedAt()); }
  61:     private static Boolean quality(TradeSignal signal) { return signal == null ? null : signal.getDataQualityValid(); }
  62:     private static Long age(Instant reference, TradeSignal signal) {
  63:         return signal == null || signal.getGeneratedAt() == null ? null : Math.max(0L, Duration.between(signal.getGeneratedAt(), reference).getSeconds());
  64:     }
  65:     private static String fiveReason(TradeSignal signal) { return signal == null ? "MISSING" : Boolean.TRUE.equals(signal.getDataQualityValid()) ? "AVAILABLE" : "QUALITY_UNKNOWN"; }
  66:     private static String trim(String value) { return value == null ? null : value.length() <= 1000 ? value : value.substring(0, 1000); }
  67: }

 405:         if (!result.allowed() || assessment.maxInitialPercent()==null) return result;
 406:         int capped=Math.min(result.positionPercent(),assessment.maxInitialPercent());

 410:     /** Used only after progressive raw-HTF checks have already failed; never changes an add decision. */
 411:     public void recordProgressiveAssessmentIfSoftBlocked(TradeSignal referenceSignal) {
 412:         if (referenceSignal == null || referenceSignal.getGeneratedAt() == null) return;
 413:         TradeSignal five = latestAtOrBefore(referenceSignal, CONFIRMATION_INTERVAL);
 414:         TradeSignal one = latestAtOrBefore(referenceSignal, TREND_INTERVAL);
 415:         if (!isFresh(five, referenceSignal.getGeneratedAt(), FIVE_MINUTE_MAX_AGE)
 416:                 || !isFresh(one, referenceSignal.getGeneratedAt(), ONE_HOUR_MAX_AGE)) return;
 417:         HigherTimeframeBuyAssessmentService.Assessment assessment = htfAssessment.assess(five, true, one, true);
 418:         if (assessment.band() == HigherTimeframeBuyAssessmentService.Band.SOFT_CONFLICT) {
 419:             audit(referenceSignal, "PROGRESSIVE_ADD", assessment, "SOFT_HTF_ADD_BLOCKED",
 420:                     "Existing raw 1h progressive-add guard blocked this add; FIX-154 audit only.");
 421:         }
 422:     }

 425:                        HigherTimeframeBuyAssessmentService.Assessment assessment, String finalResult, String reason) {
 426:         if (htfAuditStore != null) htfAuditStore.record(referenceSignal, path, assessment, finalResult, reason);
 427:     }

 432:         }

 434:         public static ValidationResult reject(String code, String explanation) {

  15: 
  16: /**
  17:  * Builds execution evidence from a sequence of already-generated 1m BUY signals.
  18:  *
  19:  * Important architecture rule: this service NEVER creates a TradeSignal.  AnalysisService
  20:  * remains the single source of truth for signals.  Consolidation is an execution-only path
  21:  * that may approve the CURRENT fresh 1m BUY after several recent BUY signals have persisted.
  22:  */
  23: @Service
  24: @RequiredArgsConstructor
  25: public class OpportunityConsolidationService {
  26: 
  27:     private static final String EXECUTION_INTERVAL = "1m";
  28:     private static final String CONFIRMATION_INTERVAL = "5m";
  29:     private static final String TREND_INTERVAL = "1h";
  30: 
  31:     private static final Duration OPPORTUNITY_WINDOW = Duration.ofMinutes(30);
  32:     private static final Duration FIVE_MINUTE_MAX_AGE = Duration.ofMinutes(20);
  33:     private static final Duration ONE_HOUR_MAX_AGE = Duration.ofHours(3);
  34: 
  35:     private static final int MIN_CONSECUTIVE_BUYS = 3;
  36:     private static final int STRONG_CONSECUTIVE_BUYS = 5;
  37:     private static final int MIN_AVERAGE_SCORE = 75;
  38:     private static final int MIN_AVERAGE_CONFIDENCE = 70;
  39: 
  40:     private final TradeSignalRepository signalRepository;
  41:     private final HigherTimeframeBuyAssessmentService htfAssessment;
  42:     @Autowired(required = false)
  43:     private ExecutionReplayScope replayScope;
  44:     @Autowired(required = false)
  45:     private BuyHtfAssessmentAuditStore htfAuditStore;
  46: 
  47:     public Assessment evaluate(TradeSignal currentSignal) {
  48:         if (currentSignal == null || currentSignal.getGeneratedAt() == null) {
  49:             return Assessment.reject("INVALID_SIGNAL", "Current execution signal is missing.");
  50:         }
  51:         if (!EXECUTION_INTERVAL.equals(currentSignal.getInterval()) || !isBullish(currentSignal.getDecision())) {
  52:             return Assessment.reject("NOT_1M_BUY", "Opportunity consolidation only evaluates fresh 1m BUY/STRONG_BUY signals.");
  53:         }
  54: 
  55:         TradeSignal fiveMinute = latestAtOrBefore(currentSignal, CONFIRMATION_INTERVAL);
  56:         TradeSignal oneHour = latestAtOrBefore(currentSignal, TREND_INTERVAL);
  57: 
  58:         if (!isFresh(fiveMinute, currentSignal.getGeneratedAt(), FIVE_MINUTE_MAX_AGE)) {
  59:             return Assessment.reject("MISSING_5M_CONTEXT", "No fresh 5m context is available for consolidation.");
  60:         }
  61:         if (!isFresh(oneHour, currentSignal.getGeneratedAt(), ONE_HOUR_MAX_AGE)) {
  62:             return Assessment.reject("MISSING_1H_CONTEXT", "No fresh 1h context is available for consolidation.");
  63:         }
  64:         HigherTimeframeBuyAssessmentService.Assessment htf=htfAssessment.assess(fiveMinute,true,oneHour,true);
  65:         if(!htf.allowed()) {
  66:             audit(currentSignal, htf, htf.code(), "FIX-154 higher-timeframe assessment rejected consolidation.");
  67:             return Assessment.reject(htf.code(), "FIX-154 higher-timeframe assessment rejected consolidation.");
  68:         }
  69:         SignalDecision fiveDecision=htf.effectiveFiveMinute();
  70:         SignalDecision oneDecision=htf.effectiveOneHour();
  71: 
  72:         List<TradeSignal> recent = replayScope != null && replayScope.active()
  73:                 ? replayScope.recent(currentSignal.getSymbol(), EXECUTION_INTERVAL, currentSignal.getGeneratedAt(), 20)
  74:                 : signalRepository.findTop20BySymbolAndIntervalOrderByGeneratedAtDesc(currentSignal.getSymbol(), EXECUTION_INTERVAL);
  75:         List<TradeSignal> consecutive = consecutiveFreshBuys(recent, currentSignal.getGeneratedAt());
  76: 
  77:         int count = consecutive.size();
  78:         if (count < MIN_CONSECUTIVE_BUYS) {
  79:             Assessment result = Assessment.building(
  80:                     count,
  81:                     averageScore(consecutive),
  82:                     averageConfidence(consecutive),
  83:                     firstScore(consecutive),
  84:                     currentSignal.getTotalScore(),
  85:                     fiveDecision,

1071:                                 + ". Capital remains deliberately small until confirmation improves.",
1072:                         e);
1073:             }
1074:             return null;
1075:         }
1076: 
1077:         if (!isSupportiveCurrentSignal(current) || !fiveSupportive || !oneHourSupportive
1078:                 || q.classification().equals("CHASE_ENTRY")) {
1079:             // FIX-154: the existing raw 1h guard remains the authority for add-ons.
1080:             // Only after it has rejected an add do we write optional audit evidence;
1081:             // this call can never change the decision and is skipped on successful paths.
1082:             if (!oneHourSupportive) {
1083:                 validationService.recordProgressiveAssessmentIfSoftBlocked(current);
1084:             }
1085:             return null;
1086:         }
1087: 
1088:         if (currentAllocationPercent < CONFIRMATION_TARGET_PERCENT) {
1089:             boolean evidenceConfirmed = e.evidenceScore() >= MIN_EVIDENCE_SCORE
1090:                     && e.averageScore() >= 65
1091:                     && e.averageConfidence() >= 65;
1092:             boolean directConfirmed = isBullish(current.getDecision())
1093:                     && current.getTotalScore() >= properties.minimumBuyScore();
1094:             if ((evidenceConfirmed || directConfirmed)
1095:                     && current.isAtrImmediateEntryAllowed()
1096:                     && e.opportunityHealth() >= 60
1097:                     && q.score() >= CONFIRMATION_MIN_ENTRY_QUALITY
1098:                     && q.rewardRisk() >= 1.15d) {
1099:                 int add = CONFIRMATION_TARGET_PERCENT - currentAllocationPercent;
1100:                 return ExecutionDecision.allow(
1101:                         "CONFIRMATION_ADD",
1102:                         "PROGRESSIVE_CONFIRMATION_ADD",
1103:                         add,
1104:                         "Progressive Position Building added " + add
1105:                                 + "% after the scout was confirmed. Entry quality=" + q.score()
1106:                                 + "/100, evidence=" + e.evidenceScore()
1107:                                 + ", health=" + e.opportunityHealth()
1108:                                 + ", 5m=" + e.fiveMinute() + ", 1h=" + e.oneHour() + ".",
1109:                         e);
1110:             }

--- src/main/java/com/crypto/execution/service/ExecutionReplayScope.java:1-240 ---
   1: package com.crypto.execution.service;
   2: 
   3: import com.crypto.domain.TradeSignal;
   4: import com.crypto.execution.domain.ExecutionOpportunity;
   5: import org.springframework.stereotype.Component;
   6: 
   7: import java.time.Instant;
   8: import java.util.ArrayList;
   9: import java.util.Comparator;
  10: import java.util.List;
  11: import java.util.Optional;
  12: import java.util.HashSet;
  13: import java.util.Set;
  14: import java.util.function.Consumer;
  15: 
  16: /**
  17:  * Thread-bound data/persistence override used only by Administration historical replay.
  18:  *
  19:  * The execution algorithms remain the production services. While this scope is active,
  20:  * their signal lookups are resolved exclusively from replay-generated signals as-of the
  21:  * current replay timestamp, and opportunity persistence is redirected to the supplied
  22:  * shadow sink. Production repositories are never read/written by the scoped execution path.
  23:  */
  24: @Component
  25: public class ExecutionReplayScope {
  26: 
  27:     /** FIX-109: Production parity is the default replay contract. Experimental mode is
  28:      * explicit so replay-only candidate rules can never contaminate a parity result. */
  29:     public enum ReplayLogicMode { PRODUCTION_PARITY, EXPERIMENTAL }
  30: 
  31:     private final ThreadLocal<State> state = new ThreadLocal<>();
  32: 
  33:     public Scope open(long runId, List<TradeSignal> replaySignals, Consumer<ExecutionOpportunity> opportunitySink) {
  34:         return open(runId, replaySignals, opportunitySink, ReplayLogicMode.PRODUCTION_PARITY);
  35:     }
  36: 
  37:     public Scope open(long runId, List<TradeSignal> replaySignals, Consumer<ExecutionOpportunity> opportunitySink,
  38:                       ReplayLogicMode logicMode) {
  39:         if (state.get() != null) throw new IllegalStateException("Execution replay scope is already active on this thread");
  40:         List<TradeSignal> immutable = replaySignals == null ? List.of() : replaySignals.stream()
  41:                 .filter(s -> s != null && s.getGeneratedAt() != null)
  42:                 .sorted(Comparator.comparing(TradeSignal::getGeneratedAt))
  43:                 .toList();
  44:         state.set(new State(runId, immutable, opportunitySink, logicMode == null ? ReplayLogicMode.PRODUCTION_PARITY : logicMode));
  45:         return new Scope();
  46:     }
  47: 
  48:     /** FIX-122: run-local executed boundaries, independent of Production ledger/history. */
  49:     public void recordStop(String symbol, long positionId, Instant at, String reason) {
  50:         if (!"STOP_LOSS".equals(reason)) return;
  51:         State s=required();
  52:         StopLossEvidencePolicy.Boundary previous=s.stops.get(symbol);
  53:         if (previous==null || !at.isBefore(previous.executedAt()))
  54:             s.stops.put(symbol,new StopLossEvidencePolicy.Boundary(positionId,at));
  55:     }
  56:     public StopLossEvidencePolicy.Boundary stopBoundary(String symbol, Instant asOf) {
  57:         StopLossEvidencePolicy.Boundary b=required().stops.get(symbol);
  58:         return b!=null && !b.executedAt().isAfter(asOf) ? b : null;
  59:     }
  60:     public Instant referenceInstant() { return required().reference; }
  61:     public boolean fix122Enabled() { return required().fix122Enabled; }
  62:     public void fix122Enabled(boolean value) { required().fix122Enabled=value; }
  63: 
  64:     public boolean active() { return state.get() != null; }
  65:     public long runId() { return required().runId; }
  66:     public ReplayLogicMode logicMode() { return required().logicMode; }
  67:     public boolean experimental() { return active() && required().logicMode == ReplayLogicMode.EXPERIMENTAL; }
  68:     public boolean productionParity() { return active() && required().logicMode == ReplayLogicMode.PRODUCTION_PARITY; }
  69: 
  70:     public void reference(Instant reference) { required().reference = reference; }
  71: 
  72:     /** FIX-056: expose the exact latest replay market-price event to the shared execution-price authority. */
  73:     public void referencePrice(String symbol, Instant observedAt, java.math.BigDecimal price) {
  74:         State s = required();
  75:         s.reference = observedAt;
  76:         s.latestPrice = new ReplayPrice(symbol, observedAt, price);
  77:     }
  78: 
  79:     public Optional<ReplayPrice> latestPrice() {
  80:         State s = required();
  81:         return Optional.ofNullable(s.latestPrice);
  82:     }
  83: 
  84:     /** FIX-112A: Replay-local execution ledger keyed to the exact triggering signal. */
  85:     public boolean entryConsumed(Long signalId) {
  86:         return active() && signalId != null && required().consumedSignalIds.contains(signalId);
  87:     }
  88: 
  89:     /** FIX-112A: record only after that exact shadow BUY (initial or add) executes. */
  90:     public void markEntryConsumed(Long signalId) {
  91:         if (signalId != null) required().consumedSignalIds.add(signalId);
  92:     }
  93: 
  94:     /**
  95:      * Adds a freshly generated replay signal to the as-of context stream. Signal generation
  96:      * uses this incrementally so the production confluence/BTC services can resolve only
  97:      * context that had genuinely been generated by the current replay timestamp.
  98:      */
  99:     public void appendSignal(TradeSignal signal) {
 100:         if (signal == null || signal.getGeneratedAt() == null) return;
 101:         State s = required();
 102:         s.signals.add(signal);
 103:         s.signals.sort(Comparator.comparing(TradeSignal::getGeneratedAt));
 104:     }
 105: 
 106:     public List<TradeSignal> recent(String symbol, String interval, Instant reference, int limit) {
 107:         State s = required();
 108:         Instant ref = reference != null ? reference : s.reference;
 109:         if (ref == null) return List.of();
 110:         return s.signals.stream()
 111:                 .filter(x -> symbol.equals(x.getSymbol()) && interval.equals(x.getInterval()))
 112:                 .filter(x -> !x.getGeneratedAt().isAfter(ref))
 113:                 .sorted(Comparator.comparing(TradeSignal::getGeneratedAt).reversed())
 114:                 .limit(limit)
 115:                 .toList();
 116:     }
 117: 
 118:     public Optional<TradeSignal> latestAtOrBefore(String symbol, String interval, Instant reference) {
 119:         return recent(symbol, interval, reference, 1).stream().findFirst();
 120:     }
 121: 
 122:     /**
 123:      * FIX-141: Replay-side equivalent of
 124:      * TradeSignalRepository#findTopBySymbolAndIntervalAndCandleOpenTimeLessThanEqualAndGeneratedAtLessThanEqualOrderByCandleOpenTimeDesc.
 125:      * Selects by candle open time (close-time ordering), not generatedAt, so Replay cannot diverge
 126:      * from Production on which BTC snapshot it picks. candleOpenTimeBound is the caller's look-ahead
 127:      * bound (reference minus the interval duration); generatedAt must also be at or before reference.
 128:      */
 129:     public Optional<TradeSignal> latestClosedAtOrBefore(String symbol, String interval, Instant reference, Instant candleOpenTimeBound) {
 130:         State s = required();
 131:         if (reference == null || candleOpenTimeBound == null) return Optional.empty();
 132:         return s.signals.stream()
 133:                 .filter(x -> symbol.equals(x.getSymbol()) && interval.equals(x.getInterval()))
 134:                 .filter(x -> x.getGeneratedAt() != null && !x.getGeneratedAt().isAfter(reference))
 135:                 .filter(x -> x.getCandleOpenTime() != null && !x.getCandleOpenTime().isAfter(candleOpenTimeBound))
 136:                 .max(Comparator.comparing(TradeSignal::getCandleOpenTime));
 137:     }
 138: 
 139:     public Optional<TradeSignal> previousBefore(String symbol, String interval, Instant reference) {
 140:         State s = required();
 141:         return s.signals.stream()
 142:                 .filter(x -> symbol.equals(x.getSymbol()) && interval.equals(x.getInterval()))
 143:                 .filter(x -> x.getGeneratedAt().isBefore(reference))
 144:                 .max(Comparator.comparing(TradeSignal::getGeneratedAt));
 145:     }
 146: 
 147:     public Optional<ExecutionOpportunity> currentOpportunity(String symbol, List<String> statuses) {
 148:         ExecutionOpportunity o = required().opportunity;
 149:         if (o == null || !symbol.equals(o.getSymbol())) return Optional.empty();
 150:         if (statuses != null && !statuses.isEmpty() && !statuses.contains(o.getStatus())) return Optional.empty();
 151:         return Optional.of(o);
 152:     }
 153: 
 154:     public ExecutionOpportunity saveOpportunity(ExecutionOpportunity opportunity) {
 155:         State s = required();
 156:         s.opportunity = opportunity;
 157:         if (s.opportunitySink != null) s.opportunitySink.accept(opportunity);
 158:         return opportunity;
 159:     }
 160: 
 161:     public final class Scope implements AutoCloseable {
 162:         private boolean closed;
 163:         @Override public void close() {
 164:             if (!closed) {
 165:                 state.remove();
 166:                 closed = true;
 167:             }
 168:         }
 169:     }
 170: 
 171:     private State required() {
 172:         State s = state.get();
 173:         if (s == null) throw new IllegalStateException("No Administration execution replay scope is active");
 174:         return s;
 175:     }
 176: 
 177:     public record ReplayPrice(String symbol, Instant observedAt, java.math.BigDecimal price) {}
 178: 
 179:     private static final class State {
 180:         final long runId;
 181:         final List<TradeSignal> signals;
 182:         final Consumer<ExecutionOpportunity> opportunitySink;
 183:         final ReplayLogicMode logicMode;
 184:         boolean fix122Enabled = true;
 185:         final java.util.Map<String,StopLossEvidencePolicy.Boundary> stops = new java.util.HashMap<>();
 186:         Instant reference;
 187:         ExecutionOpportunity opportunity;
 188:         ReplayPrice latestPrice;
 189:         final Set<Long> consumedSignalIds = new HashSet<>();
 190:         State(long runId, List<TradeSignal> signals, Consumer<ExecutionOpportunity> opportunitySink, ReplayLogicMode logicMode) {
 191:             this.runId = runId;
 192:             this.signals = new ArrayList<>(signals);
 193:             this.opportunitySink = opportunitySink;
 194:             this.logicMode = logicMode;
 195:         }
 196:     }
 197: }

 107:     private void context(String symbol, String interval, TradeSignal value) {
 108:         when(signalRepository.findTopBySymbolAndIntervalAndGeneratedAtLessThanEqualOrderByGeneratedAtDesc(
 109:                 symbol, interval, now)).thenReturn(Optional.of(value));
 110:     }
 111: 
 112:     private TradeSignal signal(Long id, String symbol, String interval, SignalDecision decision,
 113:                                Instant generatedAt, int score, int confidence) {
 114:         return TradeSignal.builder()

 116:                 .symbol(symbol)
 117:                 .interval(interval)
 118:                 .decision(decision)
 119:                 .totalScore(score)
 120:                 .confidenceScore(confidence)
 121:                 .generatedAt(generatedAt)
 122:                 .build();
 123:     }

 113:         processingStore.register(signal, origin,sourceEventId);
 114:         return signal;
 115:     }
 116: 
 117:     @Transactional
 118:     public TradeSignal analyzeRecoveredForProcessing(TechnicalIndicator indicator, Instant at) {
 119:         TradeSignal signal = analyzeRecovered(indicator, at);
 120:         // This legacy LIVE-mode recovery route is execution-capable; it must not
 121:         // retain the historical-only marker used by non-executing catch-up rows.
 122:         signal.setSignalMode("RECOVERY_EXECUTABLE");
 123:         signal = signalRepository.save(signal);
 124:         processingStore.register(signal, com.crypto.execution.processing.ProcessingOrigin.RECOVERY);
 125:         return signal;
 126:     }
 127: 
 128:     /**
 129:      * Manual entry point used by controllers or recovery jobs.
 130:      * It reads the latest already-persisted technical indicator.
 131:      */
 132:     @Transactional
 133:     public TradeSignal analyze(String symbol, String interval) {
 134:         TechnicalIndicator indicator = technicalIndicatorService
 135:                 .getLatest(symbol, interval)
 136:                 .orElseThrow(() -> new IllegalArgumentException(
 137:                         "No technical indicator found for " + symbol + " " + interval
 138:                 ));
 139: 
 140:         return analyze(indicator);
 141:     }
 142: 
 143:     /**
 144:      * Automatic entry point. It receives the TechnicalIndicator row that was
 145:      * just saved by TechnicalIndicatorService and never recalculates candles.
 146:      */
 147:     @Transactional
 148:     public TradeSignal analyze(TechnicalIndicator indicator) {
 149:         IndicatorSnapshot snapshot = toSnapshot(indicator);
 150:         TradeSignal signal = buildSignal(snapshot, Instant.now(), false, false);
 151:         // FIX-153: physical createdAt and persisted mode distinguish live latency from as-of history.
 152:         signal.setSignalMode("LIVE");
 153:         return signalRepository.save(signal);
 154:     }
 155: 
 156:     /**
 157:      * FIX-043 production recovery path. Persists a missing historical signal while forcing
 158:      * every time-sensitive context lookup (sentiment, fundamentals, MTF/BTC context, etc.)
 159:      * to the recovered candle's original close/evaluation time.
 160:      *
 161:      * This is deliberately NOT equivalent to analyze(indicator), which uses Instant.now().
 162:      * Using current context to reconstruct a 12:21 candle at 12:30 would create a synthetic
 163:      * signal that Production could never have produced at 12:21 and would break Replay parity.
 164:      * The same buildSignal(...) implementation is shared; only temporal authority differs.
 165:      */
 166:     @Transactional
 167:     public TradeSignal analyzeRecovered(TechnicalIndicator indicator, Instant evaluationTime) {
 168:         if (indicator == null) {
 169:             throw new IllegalArgumentException("Recovered technical indicator is required");
 170:         }
 171:         IndicatorSnapshot snapshot = toSnapshot(indicator);
 172:         Instant asOf = evaluationTime == null ? snapshot.candleOpenTime() : evaluationTime;
 173:         TradeSignal signal = buildSignal(snapshot, asOf, true, false);
 174:         signal.setSignalMode("HISTORICAL_ONLY");
 175:         return signalRepository.save(signal);
 176:     }
 177: 
 178:     /**
 179:      * Regression-only analysis path. Runs the same production scoring/final-decision
 180:      * code but returns an unsaved TradeSignal so replay can never write to trade_signal.
 181:      */
 182:     @Transactional(readOnly = true)
 183:     public TradeSignal analyzeForRegression(IndicatorSnapshot snapshot, Instant evaluationTime) {
 184:         if (snapshot == null) {
 185:             throw new IllegalArgumentException("Regression indicator snapshot is required");
 186:         }
 187:         ReplayAnalysisProfiler profiler = replayAnalysisProfiler.get();
 188:         long startedNs = profiler == null ? 0L : System.nanoTime();
 189:         try {
 190:             return buildSignal(snapshot, evaluationTime == null ? snapshot.candleOpenTime() : evaluationTime, true, true);
 191:         } finally {
 192:             if (profiler != null) {
 193:                 profiler.recordCall(System.nanoTime() - startedNs);
 194:             }
 195:         }
 196:     }
 197:

 105:     private static Instant candleClose(TradeSignal signal) {

 107:         long seconds = switch (signal.getInterval()) { case "1m" -> 60; case "5m" -> 300; case "1h" -> 3600; default -> 0; };
 108:         return seconds == 0 ? null : signal.getCandleOpenTime().plusSeconds(seconds).minusMillis(1);
 109:     }
