   1: package com.crypto.service;
   2: 
   3: import com.crypto.domain.TradeSignal;
   4: import com.crypto.execution.service.ExecutionReplayScope;
   5: import lombok.extern.slf4j.Slf4j;
   6: import org.springframework.beans.factory.annotation.Qualifier;
   7: import org.springframework.beans.factory.annotation.Autowired;
   8: import org.springframework.jdbc.core.JdbcTemplate;
   9: import org.springframework.stereotype.Component;
  10: 
  11: import java.sql.Timestamp;
  12: import java.time.Duration;
  13: import java.time.Instant;
  14: import java.util.concurrent.Executor;
  15: import java.util.concurrent.RejectedExecutionException;
  16: 
  17: /**
  18:  * Best-effort, append-only FIX-154 evidence. It snapshots values then writes
  19:  * on a bounded dedicated executor: an audit insert never nests a database
  20:  * connection inside an execution transaction and never changes a BUY result.
  21:  */
  22: @Slf4j
  23: @Component
  24: public class BuyHtfAssessmentAuditStore {
  25:     private final JdbcTemplate jdbc;
  26:     private final Executor executor;
  27:     private final Fix154Metrics metrics;
  28:     @Autowired(required = false)
  29:     private ExecutionReplayScope replayScope;
  30: 
  31:     public BuyHtfAssessmentAuditStore(JdbcTemplate jdbc, @Qualifier("fix154AuditExecutor") Executor executor,
  32:                                       Fix154Metrics metrics) {
  33:         this.jdbc = jdbc;
  34:         this.executor = executor;
  35:         this.metrics = metrics;
  36:     }
  37: 
  38:     public void record(TradeSignal reference, String path,
  39:                        HigherTimeframeBuyAssessmentService.Assessment assessment,
  40:                        String finalResult, String reason) {
  41:         // Replay signals are JDBC-generated and have no entity id. Replay parity
  42:         // uses its own evidence tables; never write a misleading Production audit.
  43:         if (reference == null || assessment == null || reference.getId() == null
  44:                 || (replayScope != null && replayScope.active())) return;
  45:         AuditPayload payload = AuditPayload.from(reference, path, assessment, finalResult, reason);
  46:         try {
  47:             executor.execute(() -> persist(payload));
  48:         } catch (RejectedExecutionException rejected) {
  49:             metrics.auditWriteFailed();
  50:             log.warn("FIX-154 assessment audit queue is full; BUY decision remains unchanged: path={}, referenceSignalId={}",
  51:                     path, reference.getId());
  52:         }
  53:     }
  54: 
  55:     private void persist(AuditPayload p) {
  56:         try {
  57:             jdbc.update("""
  58:                     INSERT INTO buy_htf_assessment_audit (
  59:                       reference_signal_id, evaluation_path, evaluated_at, replay_mode,
  60:                       one_hour_signal_id, one_hour_raw_decision, one_hour_effective_decision,
  61:                       one_hour_candle_close_time, one_hour_age_seconds, one_hour_data_quality_valid, one_hour_usability_reason,
  62:                       five_minute_signal_id, five_minute_raw_decision, five_minute_effective_decision,
  63:                       five_minute_candle_close_time, five_minute_age_seconds, five_minute_data_quality_valid, five_minute_usability_reason,
  64:                       confidence_threshold, assessment_band, hard_confirmation_present, max_initial_percent,
  65:                       legacy_veto_result, final_result, final_reason)
  66:                     VALUES (?, ?, ?, FALSE, ?, ?, ?, ?, ?, ?, ?, ?, ?, ?, ?, ?, ?, ?, ?, ?, ?, ?, ?, ?, ?)
  67:                     """,
  68:                     p.referenceId, p.path, Timestamp.from(p.evaluatedAt),
  69:                     p.one.id, p.one.decision, p.effectiveOne, timestamp(p.one.candleClose), p.one.ageSeconds, p.one.quality, p.one.usability,
  70:                     p.five.id, p.five.decision, p.effectiveFive, timestamp(p.five.candleClose), p.five.ageSeconds, p.five.quality, p.five.usability,
  71:                     HigherTimeframeBuyAssessmentService.HARD_CONFIDENCE_THRESHOLD, p.band, p.hardConfirmationPresent, p.maxInitialPercent,
  72:                     p.legacyVeto, p.finalResult, p.reason);
  73:         } catch (Exception e) {
  74:             metrics.auditWriteFailed();
  75:             log.warn("FIX-154 assessment audit was not recorded; BUY decision remains unchanged: path={}, referenceSignalId={}",
  76:                     p.path, p.referenceId, e);
  77:         }
  78:     }
  79: 
  80:     private static Timestamp timestamp(Instant value) { return value == null ? null : Timestamp.from(value); }
  81: 
  82:     private record Context(Long id, String decision, Instant candleClose, Long ageSeconds, Boolean quality, String usability) {
  83:         static Context from(TradeSignal signal, Instant evaluatedAt) {
  84:             if (signal == null) return new Context(null, null, null, null, null, "MISSING");
  85:             Instant close = candleClose(signal);
  86:             Long age = close == null ? null : Math.max(0L, Duration.between(close, evaluatedAt).getSeconds());
  87:             return new Context(signal.getId(), signal.getDecision() == null ? null : signal.getDecision().name(), close, age,
  88:                     signal.getDataQualityValid(), Boolean.TRUE.equals(signal.getDataQualityValid()) ? "AVAILABLE" : "QUALITY_UNKNOWN");
  89:         }
  90:     }
  91:     private record AuditPayload(Long referenceId, String path, Instant evaluatedAt, Context one, Context five,
  92:                                 String effectiveOne, String effectiveFive, String band, boolean hardConfirmationPresent,
  93:                                 Integer maxInitialPercent, String legacyVeto, String finalResult, String reason) {
  94:         static AuditPayload from(TradeSignal reference, String path, HigherTimeframeBuyAssessmentService.Assessment assessment,
  95:                                  String finalResult, String reason) {
  96:             Instant evaluated = reference.getGeneratedAt() == null ? Instant.now() : reference.getGeneratedAt();
  97:             String legacy = "5M_BEARISH_VETO".equals(assessment.code()) || "1H_BEARISH_VETO".equals(assessment.code())
  98:                     ? assessment.code() : null;
  99:             return new AuditPayload(reference.getId(), path, evaluated,
 100:                     Context.from(assessment.rawOneHour(), evaluated), Context.from(assessment.rawFiveMinute(), evaluated),
 101:                     assessment.effectiveOneHour().name(), assessment.effectiveFiveMinute().name(), assessment.band().name(),
 102:                     assessment.hardConfirmationPresent(), assessment.maxInitialPercent(), legacy, finalResult, trim(reason));
 103:         }
 104:     }
 105:     private static Instant candleClose(TradeSignal signal) {
 106:         if (signal.getCandleOpenTime() == null || signal.getInterval() == null) return null;
 107:         long seconds = switch (signal.getInterval()) { case "1m" -> 60; case "5m" -> 300; case "1h" -> 3600; default -> 0; };
 108:         return seconds == 0 ? null : signal.getCandleOpenTime().plusSeconds(seconds).minusMillis(1);
 109:     }
 110:     private static String trim(String value) { return value == null ? null : value.length() <= 1000 ? value : value.substring(0, 1000); }
 111: }

  65: 
  66:         WalletSettings settings = settings();
  67:         if (settings.isRequireNewBuyTransition()) {
  68:             TradeSignal previous = previousSignal(executionSignal.getSymbol(), EXECUTION_INTERVAL, executionSignal.getGeneratedAt()).orElse(null);
  69:             // FIX-112A: a bullish label alone is not a consumed entry. Only keep
  70:             // BUY_CONTINUATION protection when that exact previous signal has an
  71:             // executed BUY (Production) or executed shadow BUY (Replay).
  72:             if (isFresh(previous, executionSignal.getGeneratedAt(), ONE_MINUTE_TRANSITION_MAX_AGE)
  73:                     && isBullish(previous.getDecision())
  74:                     && entryConsumptionPolicy.resolve(previous.getId()) != EntryConsumptionState.NOT_CONSUMED) {
  75:                 return ValidationResult.reject("BUY_CONTINUATION",
  76:                         "BUY was not executed because a recent bullish opportunity for this symbol was already consumed. "
  77:                                 + "A new WATCH/NEUTRAL/SELL -> BUY transition is required.");
  78:             }
  79:         }
  80: 
  81:         TradeSignal fiveMinute = latestAtOrBefore(executionSignal, CONFIRMATION_INTERVAL);
  82:         if (!isFresh(fiveMinute, executionSignal.getGeneratedAt(), FIVE_MINUTE_MAX_AGE)) {
  83:             return ValidationResult.reject("MISSING_5M_CONFIRMATION",
  84:                     "No fresh 5m confirmation was available for the 1m BUY signal.");
  85:         }
  86: 
  87:         TradeSignal oneHour = latestAtOrBefore(executionSignal, TREND_INTERVAL);
  88:         if (!isFresh(oneHour, executionSignal.getGeneratedAt(), ONE_HOUR_MAX_AGE)) {
  89:             return ValidationResult.reject("MISSING_1H_CONTEXT",
  90:                     "No fresh 1h strategic context was available for the 1m BUY signal.");
  91:         }
  92: 
  93:         HigherTimeframeBuyAssessmentService.Assessment assessment=htfAssessment.assess(fiveMinute,true,oneHour,true);
  94:         if(!assessment.allowed()) {
  95:             recordAssessmentMetric(assessment);
  96:             audit(executionSignal, "DIRECT_BUY", assessment, assessment.code(), "FIX-154 higher-timeframe assessment rejected this BUY.");
  97:             return ValidationResult.reject(assessment.code(), "FIX-154 higher-timeframe assessment rejected this BUY.");
  98:         }
  99: 
 100:         ExecutionProfile profile = ExecutionProfile.from(settings.getExecutionProfile());
 101:         ValidationResult profileResult = switch (profile) {
 102:             case CONSERVATIVE -> conservativeBuy(assessment.effectiveFiveMinute(), assessment.effectiveOneHour());
 103:             // FIX-112B: only the fresh direct-BUY BALANCED path receives the narrow
 104:             // 5m-NEUTRAL exception. validateBuyContext() and AGGRESSIVE fallback keep
 105:             // calling balancedBuy() directly so their proven Production behavior is unchanged.
 106:             case BALANCED -> balancedBuyWithNeutralFiveException(
 107:                     executionSignal, assessment.effectiveFiveMinute(), assessment.effectiveOneHour(), entryQualityScore);
 108:             case AGGRESSIVE -> aggressiveBuy(executionSignal, assessment.effectiveFiveMinute(), assessment.effectiveOneHour());
 109:         };
 110:         ValidationResult result = cap(profileResult, assessment);
 111:         audit(executionSignal, "DIRECT_BUY", assessment, result.code(), result.explanation());
 112:         return result;
 113:     }
 114: 
 115:     /**
 116:      * FIX-021 / accumulated-evidence authority parity:
 117:      * Validate only the current 5m/1h execution authority using the same configured
 118:      * execution profile as a normal direct BUY. This intentionally skips the 1m BUY
 119:      * transition requirement because accumulated evidence may be triggered by a WATCH,
 120:      * but it must never be allowed to use weaker higher-timeframe authority than the
 121:      * normal BUY path would accept.
 122:      */
 123:     public ValidationResult validateBuyContext(TradeSignal referenceSignal) {
 124:         return validateBuyContext(referenceSignal, "ACCUMULATED_EVIDENCE");
 125:     }

 127:     /** The caller path is audit evidence only; context eligibility remains identical. */
 128:     public ValidationResult validateBuyContext(TradeSignal referenceSignal, String auditPath) {
 129:         if (referenceSignal == null || referenceSignal.getGeneratedAt() == null) {
 130:             return ValidationResult.reject("INVALID_SIGNAL", "Execution reference signal is missing required timing data.");
 131:         }
 132: 
 133:         TradeSignal fiveMinute = latestAtOrBefore(referenceSignal, CONFIRMATION_INTERVAL);
 134:         if (!isFresh(fiveMinute, referenceSignal.getGeneratedAt(), FIVE_MINUTE_MAX_AGE)) {
 135:             return ValidationResult.reject("MISSING_5M_CONFIRMATION",
 136:                     "No fresh 5m confirmation was available for accumulated-evidence authority.");
 137:         }
 138: 
 139:         TradeSignal oneHour = latestAtOrBefore(referenceSignal, TREND_INTERVAL);
 140:         if (!isFresh(oneHour, referenceSignal.getGeneratedAt(), ONE_HOUR_MAX_AGE)) {
 141:             return ValidationResult.reject("MISSING_1H_CONTEXT",
 142:                     "No fresh 1h strategic context was available for accumulated-evidence authority.");
 143:         }
 144:

 146:         if(!assessment.allowed()) {

 149:             return ValidationResult.reject(assessment.code(), "FIX-154 higher-timeframe assessment rejected this BUY context.");
 150:         }
 151: 
 152:         ExecutionProfile profile = ExecutionProfile.from(settings().getExecutionProfile());
 153:         TradeSignal oneMinuteQuality = latestAtOrBefore(referenceSignal, EXECUTION_INTERVAL);
 154:         TradeSignal qualitySignal = oneMinuteQuality == null ? referenceSignal : oneMinuteQuality;
 155:         ValidationResult profileResult = switch (profile) {
 156:             case CONSERVATIVE -> conservativeBuy(assessment.effectiveFiveMinute(), assessment.effectiveOneHour());
 157:             case BALANCED -> balancedBuy(assessment.effectiveFiveMinute(), assessment.effectiveOneHour());
 158:             case AGGRESSIVE -> aggressiveBuy(qualitySignal, assessment.effectiveFiveMinute(), assessment.effectiveOneHour());
 159:         };

--- src/main/java/com/crypto/service/TradeExecutionValidationService.java:400-455 ---
 400:     private WalletSettings settings() {
 401:         return settingsRepository.findById(1L)
 402:                 .orElseGet(() -> WalletSettings.builder()
 403:                         .id(1L)
 404:                         .executionProfile(ExecutionProfile.BALANCED.name())
 405:                         .requireNewBuyTransition(true)
 406:                         .dynamicProfitLockEnabled(true)
 407:                         .profitLockActivationPercent(BigDecimal.valueOf(70))
 408:                         .profitLockInitialPercent(BigDecimal.valueOf(40))
 409:                         .profitLockTrailStepPercent(BigDecimal.valueOf(10))
 410:                         .build());

 413:     private ValidationResult cap(ValidationResult result, HigherTimeframeBuyAssessmentService.Assessment assessment) {
 414:         if (!result.allowed() || assessment.maxInitialPercent()==null) return result;
 415:         int capped=Math.min(result.positionPercent(),assessment.maxInitialPercent());
 416:         if (capped != result.positionPercent() && fix154Metrics != null) fix154Metrics.softCappedEntry();
 417:         return capped==result.positionPercent()?result:ValidationResult.allow(capped,result.code(),result.explanation()+" FIX-154 soft-HTF cap="+capped+"%.");
 418:     }
 419: 
 420:     /** Used only after progressive raw-HTF checks have already failed; never changes an add decision. */
 421:     public void recordProgressiveAssessmentIfSoftBlocked(TradeSignal referenceSignal) {
 422:         if (referenceSignal == null || referenceSignal.getGeneratedAt() == null) return;
 423:         TradeSignal five = latestAtOrBefore(referenceSignal, CONFIRMATION_INTERVAL);
 424:         TradeSignal one = latestAtOrBefore(referenceSignal, TREND_INTERVAL);
 425:         if (!isFresh(five, referenceSignal.getGeneratedAt(), FIVE_MINUTE_MAX_AGE)
 426:                 || !isFresh(one, referenceSignal.getGeneratedAt(), ONE_HOUR_MAX_AGE)) return;
 427:         HigherTimeframeBuyAssessmentService.Assessment assessment = htfAssessment.assess(five, true, one, true);
 428:         if (assessment.band() == HigherTimeframeBuyAssessmentService.Band.SOFT_CONFLICT) {
 429:             if (fix154Metrics != null) fix154Metrics.softAddBlocked();
 430:             audit(referenceSignal, "PROGRESSIVE_ADD", assessment, "SOFT_HTF_ADD_BLOCKED",
 431:                     "Existing raw 1h progressive-add guard blocked this add; FIX-154 audit only.");
 432:         }
 433:     }
 434: 
 435:     private void audit(TradeSignal referenceSignal, String path,
 436:                        HigherTimeframeBuyAssessmentService.Assessment assessment, String finalResult, String reason) {
 437:         if (htfAuditStore != null) htfAuditStore.record(referenceSignal, path, assessment, finalResult, reason);
 438:     }
 439: 
 440:     private void recordAssessmentMetric(HigherTimeframeBuyAssessmentService.Assessment assessment) {
 441:         if (fix154Metrics != null && "1H_BEARISH_VETO".equals(assessment.code())
 442:                 && assessment.band() == HigherTimeframeBuyAssessmentService.Band.NOT_TRUSTED) {
 443:             fix154Metrics.untrustedBearishOneHourBlocked();
 444:         }
 445:     }

 447:     public record ValidationResult(boolean allowed, String code, String explanation, int positionPercent) {
 448:         public static ValidationResult allow(int positionPercent, String code, String explanation) {
 449:             return new ValidationResult(true, code, explanation, Math.max(1, Math.min(100, positionPercent)));
 450:         }
 451: 
 452:         public static ValidationResult reject(String code, String explanation) {
 453:             return new ValidationResult(false, code, explanation, 0);
 454:         }
 455:     }

  35:     private static final int MIN_CONSECUTIVE_BUYS = 3;
  36:     private static final int STRONG_CONSECUTIVE_BUYS = 5;
  37:     private static final int MIN_AVERAGE_SCORE = 75;
  38:     private static final int MIN_AVERAGE_CONFIDENCE = 70;
  39: 
  40:     private final TradeSignalRepository signalRepository;
  41:     private final HigherTimeframeBuyAssessmentService htfAssessment;
  42:     @Autowired(required = false)
  43:     private ExecutionReplayScope replayScope;
  44:     @Autowired(required = false)
  45:     private BuyHtfAssessmentAuditStore htfAuditStore;
  46: 
  47:     public Assessment evaluate(TradeSignal currentSignal) {
  48:         if (currentSignal == null || currentSignal.getGeneratedAt() == null) {
  49:             return Assessment.reject("INVALID_SIGNAL", "Current execution signal is missing.");
  50:         }
  51:         if (!EXECUTION_INTERVAL.equals(currentSignal.getInterval()) || !isBullish(currentSignal.getDecision())) {
  52:             return Assessment.reject("NOT_1M_BUY", "Opportunity consolidation only evaluates fresh 1m BUY/STRONG_BUY signals.");
  53:         }
  54: 
  55:         TradeSignal fiveMinute = latestAtOrBefore(currentSignal, CONFIRMATION_INTERVAL);
  56:         TradeSignal oneHour = latestAtOrBefore(currentSignal, TREND_INTERVAL);
  57: 
  58:         if (!isFresh(fiveMinute, currentSignal.getGeneratedAt(), FIVE_MINUTE_MAX_AGE)) {
  59:             return Assessment.reject("MISSING_5M_CONTEXT", "No fresh 5m context is available for consolidation.");
  60:         }
  61:         if (!isFresh(oneHour, currentSignal.getGeneratedAt(), ONE_HOUR_MAX_AGE)) {
  62:             return Assessment.reject("MISSING_1H_CONTEXT", "No fresh 1h context is available for consolidation.");
  63:         }
  64:         HigherTimeframeBuyAssessmentService.Assessment htf=htfAssessment.assess(fiveMinute,true,oneHour,true);
  65:         if(!htf.allowed()) {
  66:             audit(currentSignal, htf, htf.code(), "FIX-154 higher-timeframe assessment rejected consolidation.");
  67:             return Assessment.reject(htf.code(), "FIX-154 higher-timeframe assessment rejected consolidation.");
  68:         }
  69:         SignalDecision fiveDecision=htf.effectiveFiveMinute();
  70:         SignalDecision oneDecision=htf.effectiveOneHour();
  71: 
  72:         List<TradeSignal> recent = replayScope != null && replayScope.active()
  73:                 ? replayScope.recent(currentSignal.getSymbol(), EXECUTION_INTERVAL, currentSignal.getGeneratedAt(), 20)
  74:                 : signalRepository.findTop20BySymbolAndIntervalOrderByGeneratedAtDesc(currentSignal.getSymbol(), EXECUTION_INTERVAL);
  75:         List<TradeSignal> consecutive = consecutiveFreshBuys(recent, currentSignal.getGeneratedAt());
  76: 
  77:         int count = consecutive.size();
  78:         if (count < MIN_CONSECUTIVE_BUYS) {
  79:             Assessment result = Assessment.building(
  80:                     count,
  81:                     averageScore(consecutive),
  82:                     averageConfidence(consecutive),
  83:                     firstScore(consecutive),
  84:                     currentSignal.getTotalScore(),
  85:                     fiveDecision,
  86:                     oneDecision,
  87:                     "BUILDING",
  88:                     "Bullish opportunity is building: " + count + "/" + MIN_CONSECUTIVE_BUYS
  89:                             + " consecutive fresh 1m BUY signals."
  90:             );
  91:             audit(currentSignal, htf, result.code(), result.explanation());
  92:             return result;
  93:         }
  94: 
  95:         int averageScore = averageScore(consecutive);
  96:         int averageConfidence = averageConfidence(consecutive);
  97:         int firstScore = firstScore(consecutive);
  98:         int latestScore = currentSignal.getTotalScore();
  99:         boolean scoreStableOrImproving = latestScore >= firstScore - 3;
 100: 
 101:         if (averageScore < MIN_AVERAGE_SCORE || averageConfidence < MIN_AVERAGE_CONFIDENCE || !scoreStableOrImproving) {
 102:             Assessment result = Assessment.rejectDetailed(
 103:                     "QUALITY_DECAY",
 104:                     count,
 105:                     averageScore,
 106:                     averageConfidence,
 107:                     firstScore,
 108:                     latestScore,
 109:                     fiveDecision,
 110:                     oneDecision,
 111:                     "Persistent BUY signals exist, but quality is not stable enough. Average score=" + averageScore
 112:                             + ", average confidence=" + averageConfidence + ", first/latest score=" + firstScore + "/" + latestScore + "."
 113:             );
 114:             audit(currentSignal, htf, result.code(), result.explanation());
 115:             return result;
 116:         }
 117: 
 118:         // Fully neutral 5m + 1h needs stronger persistence; this avoids treating repeated 1m noise as confirmation.
 119:         if (fiveDecision == SignalDecision.NEUTRAL
 120:                 && oneDecision == SignalDecision.NEUTRAL
 121:                 && count < STRONG_CONSECUTIVE_BUYS) {
 122:             Assessment result = Assessment.building(
 123:                     count,
 124:                     averageScore,
 125:                     averageConfidence,
 126:                     firstScore,
 127:                     latestScore,
 128:                     fiveDecision,
 129:                     oneDecision,
 130:                     "BUILDING_STRONGER_CONFIRMATION",
 131:                     "1m BUY persistence is valid, but both 5m and 1h are NEUTRAL. Waiting for "
 132:                             + STRONG_CONSECUTIVE_BUYS + " consecutive BUY signals or improving higher-timeframe context."
 133:             );
 134:             audit(currentSignal, htf, result.code(), result.explanation());
 135:             return result;
 136:         }
 137: 
 138:         int positionPercent = consolidatedPositionPercent(count, fiveDecision, oneDecision);
 139:         if(htf.maxInitialPercent()!=null) positionPercent=Math.min(positionPercent,htf.maxInitialPercent());
 140:         String state = count >= STRONG_CONSECUTIVE_BUYS ? "CONFIRMED" : "BUILDING_CONFIDENCE";
 141:         String explanation = "Consolidated opportunity approved from the current signal #" + currentSignal.getId()
 142:                 + ": " + count + " consecutive fresh 1m BUY signals, average score=" + averageScore
 143:                 + ", average confidence=" + averageConfidence + ", score path=" + firstScore + "ƒ+'" + latestScore
 144:                 + ", 5m=" + fiveDecision + ", 1h=" + oneDecision
 145:                 + ". Reduced execution size=" + positionPercent + "%. No second trade signal was generated.";
 146: 
 147:         Assessment result = Assessment.allow(
 148:                 positionPercent,
 149:                 count,
 150:                 averageScore,
 151:                 averageConfidence,
 152:                 firstScore,
 153:                 latestScore,
 154:                 fiveDecision,
 155:                 oneDecision,
 156:                 state,
 157:                 explanation
 158:         );
 159:         audit(currentSignal, htf, result.code(), result.explanation());
 160:         return result;
 161:     }
 162: 
 163:     private void audit(TradeSignal reference, HigherTimeframeBuyAssessmentService.Assessment assessment,
 164:                        String finalResult, String reason) {
 165:         if (htfAuditStore != null) htfAuditStore.record(reference, "CONSOLIDATION", assessment, finalResult, reason);
 166:     }

 168:     private List<TradeSignal> consecutiveFreshBuys(List<TradeSignal> recent, Instant reference) {
 169:         List<TradeSignal> result = new ArrayList<>();
 170:         Instant cutoff = reference.minus(OPPORTUNITY_WINDOW);
 171:         for (TradeSignal signal : recent) {
 172:             if (signal.getGeneratedAt() == null || signal.getGeneratedAt().isAfter(reference)) {
 173:                 continue;
 174:             }
 175:             if (signal.getGeneratedAt().isBefore(cutoff)) {
 176:                 break;
 177:             }
 178:             if (!isBullish(signal.getDecision())) {
 179:                 break;
 180:             }

--- src/main/java/com/crypto/service/HigherTimeframeBuyAssessmentService.java ---
   1: package com.crypto.service;
   2: 
   3: import com.crypto.domain.SignalDecision;
   4: import com.crypto.domain.TradeSignal;
   5: import org.springframework.stereotype.Service;
   6: 
   7: /**
   8:  * Pure FIX-154 BUY-context assessment. It deliberately returns effective
   9:  * decisions separately and never mutates the JPA-managed context signals.
  10:  */
  11: @Service
  12: public class HigherTimeframeBuyAssessmentService {
  13:     public static final int HARD_CONFIDENCE_THRESHOLD = 70;
  14: 
  15:     public Assessment assess(TradeSignal fiveMinute, boolean fiveFresh,
  16:                              TradeSignal oneHour, boolean oneFresh) {
  17:         if (!fiveFresh) return Assessment.reject("MISSING_5M_CONFIRMATION", Band.NOT_APPLICABLE, fiveMinute, oneHour);
  18:         if (!oneFresh) return Assessment.reject("MISSING_1H_CONTEXT", Band.NOT_APPLICABLE, fiveMinute, oneHour);
  19: 
  20:         SignalDecision five = decision(fiveMinute);
  21:         SignalDecision one = decision(oneHour);
  22:         if (bearish(five)) {
  23:             boolean hard = hard(oneHour);
  24:             return Assessment.reject("5M_BEARISH_VETO", hard ? Band.HARD_BEARISH_CANDIDATE : band(oneHour), fiveMinute, oneHour)
  25:                     .withHardConfirmation(hard);
  26:         }
  27:         if (bearish(one) && !qualityValid(oneHour)) {
  28:             return Assessment.reject("1H_BEARISH_VETO", Band.NOT_TRUSTED, fiveMinute, oneHour);
  29:         }
  30:         if (hard(oneHour)) {
  31:             return Assessment.reject("HTF_TRUSTED_BEARISH_VETO", Band.HARD_BEARISH_CANDIDATE, fiveMinute, oneHour);
  32:         }
  33:         if (soft(oneHour)) {
  34:             return Assessment.allow(Band.SOFT_CONFLICT, five, SignalDecision.NEUTRAL, 25, fiveMinute, oneHour);
  35:         }
  36:         return Assessment.allow(qualityValid(oneHour) ? Band.NO_CONFLICT : Band.NOT_TRUSTED,
  37:                 five, one, null, fiveMinute, oneHour);
  38:     }
  39: 
  40:     private Band band(TradeSignal one) {
  41:         if (!qualityValid(one)) return Band.NOT_TRUSTED;
  42:         if (hard(one)) return Band.HARD_BEARISH_CANDIDATE;
  43:         if (soft(one)) return Band.SOFT_CONFLICT;
  44:         return Band.NO_CONFLICT;
  45:     }
  46:     private boolean hard(TradeSignal s) { return qualityValid(s) && decision(s) == SignalDecision.STRONG_SELL && confidence(s) >= HARD_CONFIDENCE_THRESHOLD; }
  47:     private boolean soft(TradeSignal s) { return qualityValid(s) && (decision(s) == SignalDecision.SELL || (decision(s) == SignalDecision.STRONG_SELL && confidence(s) < HARD_CONFIDENCE_THRESHOLD)); }
  48:     private boolean qualityValid(TradeSignal s) { return s != null && Boolean.TRUE.equals(s.getDataQualityValid()); }
  49:     private int confidence(TradeSignal s) { return s == null ? 0 : s.getConfidenceScore(); }
  50:     private SignalDecision decision(TradeSignal s) { return s == null || s.getDecision() == null ? SignalDecision.NEUTRAL : s.getDecision(); }
  51:     private boolean bearish(SignalDecision d) { return d == SignalDecision.SELL || d == SignalDecision.STRONG_SELL; }
  52: 
  53:     public enum Band { NOT_APPLICABLE, NOT_TRUSTED, SOFT_CONFLICT, HARD_BEARISH_CANDIDATE, NO_CONFLICT }
  54:     public record Assessment(boolean allowed, String code, Band band, SignalDecision effectiveFiveMinute,
  55:                              SignalDecision effectiveOneHour, Integer maxInitialPercent,
  56:                              boolean hardConfirmationPresent, TradeSignal rawFiveMinute, TradeSignal rawOneHour) {
  57:         static Assessment reject(String code, Band band, TradeSignal five, TradeSignal one) {
  58:             return new Assessment(false, code, band, decisionOf(five), decisionOf(one), null, false, five, one);
  59:         }
  60:         static Assessment allow(Band band, SignalDecision five, SignalDecision one, Integer cap, TradeSignal rawFive, TradeSignal rawOne) {
  61:             return new Assessment(true, "HTF_CONTEXT_ALLOWED", band, five, one, cap, false, rawFive, rawOne);
  62:         }
  63:         Assessment withHardConfirmation(boolean value) { return new Assessment(allowed, code, band, effectiveFiveMinute, effectiveOneHour, maxInitialPercent, value, rawFiveMinute, rawOneHour); }
  64:         private static SignalDecision decisionOf(TradeSignal s) { return s == null || s.getDecision() == null ? SignalDecision.NEUTRAL : s.getDecision(); }
  65:     }
  66: }

--- src/main/resources/db/migration/V107__fix_154_trusted_higher_timeframe_buy.sql ---
   1: -- FIX-154: immutable quality evidence belongs to each generated signal.
   2: -- NULL is intentional for signals generated before this migration.
   3: ALTER TABLE trade_signal
   4:     ADD COLUMN data_quality_valid BOOLEAN NULL;
   5: 
   6: -- Replay persists every mapped TradeSignal column by reflection. Keep its
   7: -- production-shaped table in lockstep, including the two earlier columns that
   8: -- were added only to trade_signal by V97/V106.
   9: ALTER TABLE trade_signal_test
  10:     ADD COLUMN btc_context_candle_close_time TIMESTAMP(6) NULL,
  11:     ADD COLUMN btc_context_age_seconds BIGINT NULL,
  12:     ADD COLUMN btc_context_freshness_threshold_seconds BIGINT NULL,
  13:     ADD COLUMN signal_mode VARCHAR(24) NULL,
  14:     ADD COLUMN data_quality_valid BOOLEAN NULL;
  15: 
  16: -- Execution-time evidence is append-only: the same reference signal can be
  17: -- assessed repeatedly through different or repeated authority paths.
  18: CREATE TABLE buy_htf_assessment_audit (
  19:     id BIGINT NOT NULL AUTO_INCREMENT PRIMARY KEY,
  20:     reference_signal_id BIGINT NOT NULL,
  21:     evaluation_path VARCHAR(40) NOT NULL,
  22:     evaluated_at TIMESTAMP(6) NOT NULL,
  23:     replay_mode BOOLEAN NOT NULL DEFAULT FALSE,
  24:     one_hour_signal_id BIGINT NULL,
  25:     one_hour_raw_decision VARCHAR(30) NULL,
  26:     one_hour_effective_decision VARCHAR(30) NULL,
  27:     one_hour_candle_close_time TIMESTAMP(6) NULL,
  28:     one_hour_age_seconds BIGINT NULL,
  29:     one_hour_data_quality_valid BOOLEAN NULL,
  30:     one_hour_usability_reason VARCHAR(96) NULL,
  31:     five_minute_signal_id BIGINT NULL,
  32:     five_minute_raw_decision VARCHAR(30) NULL,
  33:     five_minute_effective_decision VARCHAR(30) NULL,
  34:     five_minute_candle_close_time TIMESTAMP(6) NULL,
  35:     five_minute_age_seconds BIGINT NULL,
  36:     five_minute_data_quality_valid BOOLEAN NULL,
  37:     five_minute_usability_reason VARCHAR(96) NULL,
  38:     confidence_threshold INT NOT NULL,
  39:     assessment_band VARCHAR(40) NOT NULL,
  40:     hard_confirmation_present BOOLEAN NOT NULL DEFAULT FALSE,
  41:     max_initial_percent INT NULL,
  42:     legacy_veto_result VARCHAR(64) NULL,
  43:     final_result VARCHAR(96) NOT NULL,
  44:     final_reason VARCHAR(1000) NULL,
  45:     created_at TIMESTAMP(6) NOT NULL DEFAULT CURRENT_TIMESTAMP(6),
  46:     KEY idx_fix154_audit_reference (reference_signal_id, evaluated_at),
  47:     KEY idx_fix154_audit_path_time (evaluation_path, evaluated_at),
  48:     KEY idx_fix154_audit_band_time (assessment_band, created_at)
  49: ) ENGINE=InnoDB DEFAULT CHARSET=utf8mb4 COLLATE=utf8mb4_unicode_ci;

   1: package com.crypto.service;

   7: 
   8: /** Small in-memory operational counters; they never gate trading. */
   9: @Component
  10: public class Fix154Metrics {
  11:     private final AtomicLong untrustedBearishOneHourBlocks = new AtomicLong();
  12:     private final AtomicLong auditWriteFailures = new AtomicLong();
  13:     private final AtomicLong softCappedEntries = new AtomicLong();
  14:     private final AtomicLong softAddBlocks = new AtomicLong();
  15: 
  16:     public void untrustedBearishOneHourBlocked() { untrustedBearishOneHourBlocks.incrementAndGet(); }
  17:     public void auditWriteFailed() { auditWriteFailures.incrementAndGet(); }
  18:     public void softCappedEntry() { softCappedEntries.incrementAndGet(); }
  19:     public void softAddBlocked() { softAddBlocks.incrementAndGet(); }
  20:     public Map<String, Long> snapshot() {
  21:         return Map.of("untrusted_bearish_1h_block_count", untrustedBearishOneHourBlocks.get(),
  22:                 "audit_write_failure_count", auditWriteFailures.get(),
  23:                 "soft_capped_entry_count", softCappedEntries.get(),
  24:                 "soft_add_blocked_count", softAddBlocks.get());
  25:     }
  26: }

   7: 
   8: import java.util.concurrent.Executor;
   9: import java.util.concurrent.ThreadPoolExecutor;
  10: 
  11: /** Audit evidence must never borrow the connection held by an execution path. */
  12: @Configuration
  13: public class Fix154AuditAsyncConfig {
  14:     @Bean(name = "fix154AuditExecutor")
  15:     public Executor fix154AuditExecutor(@Value("${fix154.audit.queue-capacity:500}") int queueCapacity) {
  16:         var executor = new ThreadPoolTaskExecutor();
  17:         executor.setThreadNamePrefix("fix154-audit-");
  18:         executor.setCorePoolSize(1);
  19:         executor.setMaxPoolSize(1);
  20:         executor.setQueueCapacity(Math.max(1, queueCapacity));
  21:         executor.setRejectedExecutionHandler(new ThreadPoolExecutor.AbortPolicy());
  22:         executor.initialize();
  23:         return executor;
  24:     }
  25: }

--- AGENTS.md:202-225 ---
 202: ## FIX-154: pending higher-timeframe BUY validation
 203: 
 204: FIX-154 is a separate, not-yet-implemented design. Do not merge it into the
 205: local-candle change or describe it as deployed.
 206: 
 207: Purpose: a historical bearish 1h signal may block or delay a current 1m BUY
 208: only when its own evidence is fresh, data-quality-valid, and sufficiently
 209: strong. A historical `btcContextEntryAllowed=false` must remain investigatory
 210: evidence, not a higher-timeframe validity gate; the current 1m signal's own
 211: BTC protection remains unchanged.
 212: 
 213: - Score usable bearish 1h evidence into `NOT_TRUSTED`, `SOFT_CONFLICT`, or
 214:   `HARD_BEARISH_CANDIDATE` using closed-candle local indicators.
 215: - A hard veto requires strong 1h evidence plus fresh, quality-valid bearish
 216:   5m confirmation. Stale, invalid, or unknown historical quality can never
 217:   hard-veto.
 218: - Persist `trade_signal.data_quality_valid` from
 219:   `MarketContextSnapshot.dataValid()` for every new signal. Historical NULL
 220:   means unknown/invalid; never backfill it to true.
 221: - A confirmed 1h recovery may release only a soft conflict and only with fresh,
 222:   quality-valid, non-bearish 5m evidence.
 223: - Preserve the legacy `5M_BEARISH_VETO` behavior: the new data-quality gate
 224:   applies to the new hard-veto/recovery paths, not that legacy fallback.
 225: - Add immutable BUY-evaluation audit evidence; maintain Replay parity and

--- codex-docs/ARCHITECTURE.md:1-22 ---
   1: # Crypto AI Trader ƒ?" Architecture

  18: and is not part of FIX-153 deployment scope.

--- codex-docs/ARCHITECTURE.md:178-205 ---
 178: The target runtime also requires exactly one active trader process. This is an
 179: operational prerequisite, not a database lease or fence.
 180: 
 181: ## Pending: FIX-154 higher-timeframe BUY validation
 182: 
 183: FIX-154 is separate from the local-candle change and is not implemented.
 184: 
 185: Its purpose is to let a historical bearish 1h signal affect a current 1m BUY
 186: only when the historical evidence is fresh, quality-valid, and sufficiently
 187: strong. `btcContextEntryAllowed=false` on a historical signal is investigatory
 188: evidence, not a higher-timeframe quality gate; the current 1m BTC protection
 189: remains unchanged.
 190: 
 191: The design includes reliability bands (`NOT_TRUSTED`, `SOFT_CONFLICT`,
 192: `HARD_BEARISH_CANDIDATE`), required fresh/quality-valid bearish 5m support for
 193: a hard veto, persisted `trade_signal.data_quality_valid`, recovery release
 194: rules, immutable outcome audit evidence, and Replay/no-look-ahead parity.
 195: 
 196: The implementation design is maintained in `codex-docs/FIX-154_TECHNICAL_DESIGN.md`.
 197: It supersedes no existing deployed behavior and is deliberately separate from
 198: the unrelated legacy readiness document under `src/main/resources/md/FIX-146.md`.
 199: 
 200: ## Deployment posture
 201: 
 202: Neither FIX-153 nor FIX-154 is deployment-ready. FIX-153 source hardening is
 203: implemented but unverified; FIX-154 remains pending.
 204: 
 205: Required validation includes Java 21 build/tests, disposable MySQL bootstrap,

--- codex-docs/ARCHITECTURE.md:250-285 ---
 250: terminal analysis evidence. V108 therefore ships with the FIX-154 release even
 251: though local-candle reconciliation remains disabled. `CandleAnalysisOutcomeSchemaVerifier`
 252: runs after Flyway and fails application startup with a clear message if the
 253: table is absent; this prevents later processing transactions from failing only
 254: when they attempt their first outcome write.
 255: # FIX-154 implementation update ƒ?" 2026-10-10
 256: 
 257: `HigherTimeframeBuyAssessmentService` is the single pure assessment boundary for
 258: the 5m/1h BUY context. It returns effective decisions separately from the JPA
 259: entities, so a SOFT 1h conflict can be assessed as effective `NEUTRAL` without
 260: ever changing the persisted signal. `TradeExecutionValidationService` uses it
 261: for direct BUY and accumulated-evidence authority; `OpportunityConsolidationService`
 262: uses the same assessment for consolidation. A trusted SOFT bearish 1h limits an
 263: initial entry to 25%; a trusted HARD or unknown bearish 1h rejects it, while
 264: the legacy 5m bearish veto keeps priority.
 265: 
 266: `buy_htf_assessment_audit` is append-only operational evidence. Its writer is
 267: best-effort and catches every insert failure: auditing is never part of a BUY
 268: decision or execution transaction. The progressive add route retains its
 269: existing raw-1h guard; it writes `SOFT_HTF_ADD_BLOCKED` evidence only after
 270: that guard has already rejected an add.

--- codex-docs/CHANGELOG_AI.md:235-280 ---
 235: - Defined assessment-level `maxInitialPercent=25` for SOFT outcomes and required
 236:   every self-sizing caller to apply it. Kept progressive raw-1h support checks
 237:   unchanged; they already block add stages for bearish 1h evidence.
 238: - Corrected unknown-bearish/5m-veto priority and added SOFT counters.
 239: - Design only: no Java, migration, or runtime behavior changed.
 240: # 2026-10-10 ƒ?" FIX-154 trusted higher-timeframe BUY assessment (implementation)
 241: 
 242: - **Problem:** every bearish 1h row had equal veto authority, including
 243:   historical rows with unknown data-quality provenance; there was no durable
 244:   explanation of the effective 5m/1h BUY context.
 245: - **Root cause:** BUY validation and consolidation read raw `TradeSignal`
 246:   decisions directly, with no assessment of quality or bearish strength.
 247: - **Solution:** added `data_quality_valid` persistence to `TradeSignal` and
 248:   `AnalysisService`; introduced `HigherTimeframeBuyAssessmentService`; wired it
 249:   into direct BUY, accumulated-evidence authority, consolidation and
 250:   observational progressive-add evidence. The SOFT 1h result is capped at 25%
 251:   and uses immutable effective decisions; HARD and unknown bearish evidence
 252:   vetoes. `V107` creates `data_quality_valid` and append-only
 253:   `buy_htf_assessment_audit`.
 254: - **Files/classes:** `TradeSignal`, `AnalysisService`,
 255:   `HigherTimeframeBuyAssessmentService`, `TradeExecutionValidationService`,
 256:   `OpportunityConsolidationService`, `ExecutionIntelligenceService`,
 257:   `BuyHtfAssessmentAuditStore`, `V107__fix_154_trusted_higher_timeframe_buy.sql`,
 258:   and focused tests.
 259: - **Database impact:** new nullable `trade_signal.data_quality_valid`; no
 260:   backfill. Historical `NULL` stays unknown. New append-only audit table.
 261: - **Production / Replay impact:** the same shared validation service is used
 262:   for both. Audit failure is explicitly non-blocking. V108 ships because live
 263:   runtime outcome writers require its schema; reconciliation remains disabled.
 264: - **Validation status:** focused Maven tests could not run in this shell because
 265:   the active JDK reports `release version 21 not supported`; execute with the
 266:   project Java 21 toolchain before release.
 267: 
 268: ## Validation correction ƒ?" 2026-10-10
 269: 
 270: The shell was switched to the installed Java 21 toolchain
 271: (`C:\Program Files\Java\jdk-21`). Focused Maven verification then passed:
 272: `HigherTimeframeBuyAssessmentServiceTest`,
 273: `TradeExecutionValidationServiceTest`, and
 274: `OpportunityConsolidationServiceTest`.

--- codex-docs/TRADING_RULES.md:1-120 ---
   1: # FIX-154 higher-timeframe BUY rule ƒ?" 2026-10-10
   2: 
   3: - `data_quality_valid=true` is required before a bearish 1h signal can be
   4:   treated as trusted. `NULL` is unknown and fails closed for bearish evidence.
   5: - A trusted `STRONG_SELL` at `confidence_score >= 70` blocks the BUY.
   6: - A trusted `SELL`, or trusted `STRONG_SELL` below 70, is a SOFT conflict:
   7:   profiles receive effective 1h `NEUTRAL`, and any allowed initial BUY is
   8:   capped at 25%.
   9: - A bearish 5m still returns `5M_BEARISH_VETO` before any 1h result.
  10: - Existing progressive/add rules retain their raw 1h requirement; a SOFT 1h
  11:   never authorizes an add.

--- codex-docs/FIX-154_TECHNICAL_DESIGN.md:1-25 ---
   1: # FIX-154 ƒ?" Trusted higher-timeframe BUY validation
   2: 
   3: Status: implementation design approved. FIX-154 is scheduled before the
   4: remaining FIX-153 runtime release work.
   5: 
   6: ## Purpose and boundary
   7: 
   8: FIX-154 replaces the current binary fresh-1h bearish veto with a trusted
   9: higher-timeframe assessment. It does not relax the existing 5m confirmation
  10: requirement, risk/reward result, current-1m BTC protection, or profile rules
  11: except where a raw 1h decision would otherwise act as an untrusted veto.
  12: 
  13: The legacy `src/main/resources/md/FIX-146.md` is an unrelated shared-market
  14: readiness design and is not an implementation source for this fix.
  15: 
  16: ## Current integration points to replace
  17: 
  18: The 1h/5m blocks in all three paths below must be replaced as a whole by one
  19: shared assessment call. Do not add this decision to `FinalDecisionService`.
  20: 
  21: 1. `TradeExecutionValidationService.validateBuy()` ƒ?" direct 1m BUYs.
  22: 2. `TradeExecutionValidationService.validateBuyContext()` ƒ?" accumulated/setup
  23:    authority, whose reference may be a 5m signal.
  24: 3. `OpportunityConsolidationService.evaluate()` ƒ?" consolidated BUYs.
  25: 

--- codex-docs/FIX-154_TECHNICAL_DESIGN.md:285-315 ---
 285: ## Implementation completion record ƒ?" 2026-10-10
 286: 
 287: The approved FIX-154 design is now implemented in the working tree.
 288: 
 289: - `V107__fix_154_trusted_higher_timeframe_buy.sql` creates the nullable signal
 290:   quality evidence and append-only assessment audit table. No historical value
 291:   is fabricated or backfilled.
 292: - `AnalysisService` persists `MarketContextSnapshot.dataValid()` for every new
 293:   signal.
 294: - `HigherTimeframeBuyAssessmentService` owns the hard/soft/untrusted decision
 295:   matrix. It returns immutable effective decisions and does not mutate a JPA
 296:   signal.
 297: - Direct BUY validation, accumulated-evidence authority, and opportunity
 298:   consolidation invoke that one service. A SOFT result uses effective 1h
 299:   `NEUTRAL` and applies the 25% initial-position cap. The legacy bearish-5m
 300:   veto retains precedence. Progressive adds retain their existing raw 1h
 301:   requirement and can only create best-effort audit evidence after rejection.
 302: - `BuyHtfAssessmentAuditStore` is append-only and runs in an independent
 303:   transaction. It catches insert failures so audit availability cannot alter a
 304:   business decision.
 305: 
 306: Focused Java 21 verification passed:
 307: `HigherTimeframeBuyAssessmentServiceTest`,
 308: `TradeExecutionValidationServiceTest`, and
 309: `OpportunityConsolidationServiceTest`.
 310: 
 311: This is implementation completion, not production deployment approval. Apply
 312: V107/V108 only through the normal clean Java 21 release pipeline and complete
 313: the required migration, Replay-parity, and broader regression validation before
 314: enabling it in Production.

   8:  * Runs during bean construction, before WebSocket admission and scheduled work.

  22:         } catch (Exception e) {
  23:             throw new IllegalStateException("FIX-154 requires buy_htf_assessment_audit. Apply Flyway V107 before starting.", e);
  24:         }
  25:     }
  26: 
  27:     private static void requireColumn(JdbcTemplate jdbc, String table, String column) {
  28:         Long count = jdbc.queryForObject("""
  29:                 SELECT COUNT(*) FROM information_schema.columns
  30:                 WHERE table_schema = DATABASE() AND table_name = ? AND column_name = ?
  31:                 """, Long.class, table, column);
  32:         if (count == null || count != 1) {
  33:             throw new IllegalStateException("FIX-154 requires " + table + "." + column + ". Apply Flyway V107 before starting.");
  34:         }
  35:     }

  47:     public Assessment evaluate(TradeSignal currentSignal) {
  48:         if (currentSignal == null || currentSignal.getGeneratedAt() == null) {
  49:             return Assessment.reject("INVALID_SIGNAL", "Current execution signal is missing.");
  50:         }
  51:         if (!EXECUTION_INTERVAL.equals(currentSignal.getInterval()) || !isBullish(currentSignal.getDecision())) {
  52:             return Assessment.reject("NOT_1M_BUY", "Opportunity consolidation only evaluates fresh 1m BUY/STRONG_BUY signals.");
  53:         }
  54: 
  55:         TradeSignal fiveMinute = latestAtOrBefore(currentSignal, CONFIRMATION_INTERVAL);
  56:         TradeSignal oneHour = latestAtOrBefore(currentSignal, TREND_INTERVAL);
  57: 
  58:         if (!isFresh(fiveMinute, currentSignal.getGeneratedAt(), FIVE_MINUTE_MAX_AGE)) {
  59:             return Assessment.reject("MISSING_5M_CONTEXT", "No fresh 5m context is available for consolidation.");
  60:         }
  61:         if (!isFresh(oneHour, currentSignal.getGeneratedAt(), ONE_HOUR_MAX_AGE)) {
  62:             return Assessment.reject("MISSING_1H_CONTEXT", "No fresh 1h context is available for consolidation.");
  63:         }
  64:         HigherTimeframeBuyAssessmentService.Assessment htf=htfAssessment.assess(fiveMinute,true,oneHour,true);
  65:         if(!htf.allowed()) {
  66:             audit(currentSignal, htf, htf.code(), "FIX-154 higher-timeframe assessment rejected consolidation.");
  67:             return Assessment.reject(htf.code(), "FIX-154 higher-timeframe assessment rejected consolidation.");
  68:         }
  69:         SignalDecision fiveDecision=htf.effectiveFiveMinute();
  70:         SignalDecision oneDecision=htf.effectiveOneHour();
  71:

  73:                 ? replayScope.recent(currentSignal.getSymbol(), EXECUTION_INTERVAL, currentSignal.getGeneratedAt(), 20)
  74:                 : signalRepository.findTop20BySymbolAndIntervalOrderByGeneratedAtDesc(currentSignal.getSymbol(), EXECUTION_INTERVAL);
  75:         List<TradeSignal> consecutive = consecutiveFreshBuys(recent, currentSignal.getGeneratedAt());

 168:     private List<TradeSignal> consecutiveFreshBuys(List<TradeSignal> recent, Instant reference) {
 169:         List<TradeSignal> result = new ArrayList<>();
 170:         Instant cutoff = reference.minus(OPPORTUNITY_WINDOW);
 171:         for (TradeSignal signal : recent) {
 172:             if (signal.getGeneratedAt() == null || signal.getGeneratedAt().isAfter(reference)) {
 173:                 continue;
 174:             }
 175:             if (signal.getGeneratedAt().isBefore(cutoff)) {
 176:                 break;
 177:             }
 178:             if (!isBullish(signal.getDecision())) {
 179:                 break;
 180:             }
 181:             result.add(signal);
 182:         }
 183:         return result;
 184:     }

 186:     private int consolidatedPositionPercent(int count, SignalDecision fiveMinute, SignalDecision oneHour) {
 187:         int percent = count >= STRONG_CONSECUTIVE_BUYS ? 50 : 25;
 188:         if (fiveMinute == SignalDecision.WATCH) percent += 10;
 189:         if (isBullish(fiveMinute)) percent += 20;
 190:         if (oneHour == SignalDecision.WATCH) percent += 5;
 191:         if (isBullish(oneHour)) percent += 10;
 192:         return Math.min(75, percent);
 193:     }

 195:     private int averageScore(List<TradeSignal> signals) {
 196:         if (signals.isEmpty()) return 0;
 197:         return (int) Math.round(signals.stream().mapToInt(TradeSignal::getTotalScore).average().orElse(0));
 198:     }

 200:     private int averageConfidence(List<TradeSignal> signals) {
 201:         if (signals.isEmpty()) return 0;
 202:         return (int) Math.round(signals.stream().mapToInt(TradeSignal::getConfidenceScore).average().orElse(0));
 203:     }

## FINDINGS

- **PROJECT EVIDENCE:** FIX-154 is materially present in the working tree: V107 adds nullable `trade_signal.data_quality_valid` and the audit table; `AnalysisService` writes `MarketContextSnapshot.dataValid()`; the shared assessment service is called by direct BUY, BUY-context, and consolidation paths. [V107](/C:/workspace/crypto-ai-trader-mysql/src/main/resources/db/migration/V107__fix_154_trusted_higher_timeframe_buy.sql:3) [AnalysisService](/C:/workspace/crypto-ai-trader-mysql/src/main/java/com/crypto/service/AnalysisService.java:406) [validation](/C:/workspace/crypto-ai-trader-mysql/src/main/java/com/crypto/service/TradeExecutionValidationService.java:81) [consolidation](/C:/workspace/crypto-ai-trader-mysql/src/main/java/com/crypto/service/OpportunityConsolidationService.java:55)

- **PROJECT EVIDENCE:** Historical `data_quality_valid` remains `NULL`: V107 is an `ALTER ... ADD COLUMN ... NULL` with no backfill, and the entity uses nullable `Boolean`. [migration](/C:/workspace/crypto-ai-trader-mysql/src/main/resources/db/migration/V107__fix_154_trusted_higher_timeframe_buy.sql:1) [entity](/C:/workspace/crypto-ai-trader-mysql/src/main/java/com/crypto/domain/TradeSignal.java:71)

- **PROJECT EVIDENCE:** The assessment correctly keeps raw 1h/5m entities immutable, maps a trusted SOFT 1h to effective `NEUTRAL`, caps initial sizing at 25%, and preserves raw bearish-5m veto precedence. [assessment](/C:/workspace/crypto-ai-trader-mysql/src/main/java/com/crypto/service/HigherTimeframeBuyAssessmentService.java:22) [cap](/C:/workspace/crypto-ai-trader-mysql/src/main/java/com/crypto/service/TradeExecutionValidationService.java:413) [consolidation cap](/C:/workspace/crypto-ai-trader-mysql/src/main/java/com/crypto/service/OpportunityConsolidationService.java:138)

- **PROJECT EVIDENCE:** `btcContextEntryAllowed` is not read by `HigherTimeframeBuyAssessmentService`, `TradeExecutionValidationService`, or `OpportunityConsolidationService`; therefore it is not an HTF gate in the new assessment. It remains used in `ExecutionIntelligenceService` current-signal guards. [HTF assessment](/C:/workspace/crypto-ai-trader-mysql/src/main/java/com/crypto/service/HigherTimeframeBuyAssessmentService.java:15) [current-signal guard](/C:/workspace/crypto-ai-trader-mysql/src/main/java/com/crypto/execution/service/ExecutionIntelligenceService.java:357)

- **PROJECT EVIDENCE:** The implementation diverges materially from the design’s 5m-quality rule. `hard(oneHour)` checks only the 1h row; a bearish 5m with false/NULL quality still sets `hardConfirmationPresent=true`, and a non-bearish false/NULL-quality 5m can release a SOFT 1h conflict. [assessment](/C:/workspace/crypto-ai-trader-mysql/src/main/java/com/crypto/service/HigherTimeframeBuyAssessmentService.java:22) This conflicts with the design’s requirement that invalid/unknown 5m quality cannot support a hard-veto confirmation or SOFT release. [design](/C:/workspace/crypto-ai-trader-mysql/codex-docs/FIX-154_TECHNICAL_DESIGN.md:105)

- **PROJECT EVIDENCE:** Selection and freshness use only `generatedAt`, although the design requires both generation time and candle close time to be at/before the reference signal. Existing repository and replay APIs support closed-candle selection, but FIX-154 call sites do not use them. [validation selection](/C:/workspace/crypto-ai-trader-mysql/src/main/java/com/crypto/service/TradeExecutionValidationService.java:367) [repository capability](/C:/workspace/crypto-ai-trader-mysql/src/main/java/com/crypto/repository/TradeSignalRepository.java:31) [replay capability](/C:/workspace/crypto-ai-trader-mysql/src/main/java/com/crypto/execution/service/ExecutionReplayScope.java:122)

- **AGENT INFERENCE:** This leaves the required no-look-ahead invariant structurally unenforced: a row whose `generatedAt` is eligible but whose candle has not closed is not excluded by FIX-154 selection/freshness code.

- **PROJECT EVIDENCE:** Audit persistence is best-effort and append-only in Production, but Replay audits are skipped and the SQL literal always writes `replay_mode=FALSE`. [audit store](/C:/workspace/crypto-ai-trader-mysql/src/main/java/com/crypto/service/BuyHtfAssessmentAuditStore.java:38) This does not meet the design’s equivalent Production/Replay audit-evidence requirement.

- **PROJECT EVIDENCE:** Metrics exist only as in-memory aggregate counters. No health endpoint exposes `Fix154Metrics.snapshot()`, the counters are not grouped by execution path, and consolidation does not increment its untrusted-block or soft-cap counters. [metrics](/C:/workspace/crypto-ai-trader-mysql/src/main/java/com/crypto/service/Fix154Metrics.java:10) [validation-only increments](/C:/workspace/crypto-ai-trader-mysql/src/main/java/com/crypto/service/TradeExecutionValidationService.java:413) [consolidation](/C:/workspace/crypto-ai-trader-mysql/src/main/java/com/crypto/service/OpportunityConsolidationService.java:40)

- **PROJECT EVIDENCE:** The hard-confidence threshold is a Java constant, not the named reviewed configuration requested by the design. The only FIX-154 property is audit queue capacity. [constant](/C:/workspace/crypto-ai-trader-mysql/src/main/java/com/crypto/service/HigherTimeframeBuyAssessmentService.java:13) [property](/C:/workspace/crypto-ai-trader-mysql/src/main/java/com/crypto/config/Fix154AuditAsyncConfig.java:15)

- **PROJECT EVIDENCE:** FIX-154 now has a `signal_mode` dependency despite the design addendum prohibiting one: V107 adds `trade_signal_test.signal_mode` and the startup checker requires it. [migration](/C:/workspace/crypto-ai-trader-mysql/src/main/resources/db/migration/V107__fix_154_trusted_higher_timeframe_buy.sql:9) [startup check](/C:/workspace/crypto-ai-trader-mysql/src/main/java/com/crypto/service/Fix154RuntimeSchemaChecks.java:15) [design addendum](/C:/workspace/crypto-ai-trader-mysql/codex-docs/FIX-154_TECHNICAL_DESIGN.md:249)

- **PROJECT EVIDENCE:** Audit path names diverge from the design registry: actual values include `ACCUMULATED_EVIDENCE`, `SETUP_WAKEUP`, and generic `PROGRESSIVE_ADD`, rather than the specified `BUY_CONTEXT`, `PROGRESSIVE_TREND_ADD`, `CONFIRMATION_ADD`, and `ACCUMULATED_EVIDENCE_ADD`. [context default](/C:/workspace/crypto-ai-trader-mysql/src/main/java/com/crypto/service/TradeExecutionValidationService.java:123) [setup caller](/C:/workspace/crypto-ai-trader-mysql/src/main/java/com/crypto/execution/service/ExecutionIntelligenceService.java:435) [progressive audit](/C:/workspace/crypto-ai-trader-mysql/src/main/java/com/crypto/service/TradeExecutionValidationService.java:420)

- **PROJECT EVIDENCE:** The progressive scout path blocks on raw 1h support but does not call the observational audit method on that rejection; only the already-allocated/add branch does. [scout path](/C:/workspace/crypto-ai-trader-mysql/src/main/java/com/crypto/execution/service/ExecutionIntelligenceService.java:1047) [add path](/C:/workspace/crypto-ai-trader-mysql/src/main/java/com/crypto/execution/service/ExecutionIntelligenceService.java:1077)

## OBJECTIONS

- **PROJECT EVIDENCE:** The 5m-quality omission is a material policy defect versus the design: it can label invalid 5m bearish evidence as `HARD_CONFIRMATION_PRESENT` and can permit a SOFT relaxation without valid 5m evidence.

- **PROJECT EVIDENCE:** Generated-time-only selection is insufficient for the stated closed-candle/no-look-ahead contract, even though the repository already contains a suitable close-time query.

- **PROJECT EVIDENCE:** Replay cannot produce the mandated audit row because the audit store returns immediately while Replay is active; consequently, `replay_mode` is schema-only in practice.

- **PROJECT EVIDENCE:** Documentation is internally inconsistent and does not accurately state the current working tree:
  - AGENTS.md still says FIX-154 is not implemented. [AGENTS.md](/C:/workspace/crypto-ai-trader-mysql/AGENTS.md:202)
  - Architecture says both “not implemented/pending” and, later, that the implementation exists. [architecture](/C:/workspace/crypto-ai-trader-mysql/codex-docs/ARCHITECTURE.md:17) [later update](/C:/workspace/crypto-ai-trader-mysql/codex-docs/ARCHITECTURE.md:255)
  - Changelog and trading rules report implementation, but omit the proven selection, replay-audit, 5m-quality, and signal-mode gaps. [changelog](/C:/workspace/crypto-ai-trader-mysql/codex-docs/CHANGELOG_AI.md:240) [rules](/C:/workspace/crypto-ai-trader-mysql/codex-docs/TRADING_RULES.md:1)

## UNKNOWNS

- **PROJECT EVIDENCE:** I did not access Production, query either database, run tests, modify files, or inspect the other agent’s findings.

- **AGENT INFERENCE:** There is no evidence in this read-only working-tree review that FIX-154 is Production-deployed. There is local-only migration-log evidence, but deployment status cannot be established from source and a localhost log.

- **AGENT INFERENCE:** The review is complete for the current working-tree implementation, but not a build, migration, Replay, or Production-validation result.
